class DB:
    def __init__(self, path="lumenforge.db"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self.init()
    def init(self):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commands (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            ts TEXT,
            device_address TEXT,
            char_uuid TEXT,
            hex TEXT,
            label TEXT,
            notes TEXT
        )""")
        cur.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS remote_buttons (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            label TEXT,
            hex TEXT,
            char_uuid TEXT,
            device_address TEXT,
            group_name TEXT,
            sort_order INTEGER
        )""")
        self.conn.commit()

    def get_commands(...)
    def add_remote_button(...)
    def get_remote_buttons(...)

async def scan(self):
    self.status_label.setText("Scanning...")
    devices = await BleakScanner.discover(timeout=5.0)
    self.device_list.clear()
    for d in devices:
        self.device_list.addItem(f"{d.name or 'Unknown'} | {d.address}")
    self.status_label.setText(f"Found {len(devices)} devices")

async def connect_selected(self):
    item = self.device_list.currentItem()
    if not item: return
    address = item.text().split(" | ")[-1]
    self.client = BleakClient(address)
    await self.client.connect()
    self.status_label.setText(f"Connected to {address}")
    self.char_combo.clear()
    for service in self.client.services:
        for char in service.characteristics:
            self.char_combo.addItem(f"{char.uuid} | {char.properties}", char.uuid)

DB methods:

python
class DB:
    def __init__(self, path="lumenforge.db"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self._init()
    def _init(self):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commands (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            ts TEXT NOT NULL,
            device_address TEXT,
            char_uuid TEXT,
            hex TEXT NOT NULL,
            label TEXT,
            notes TEXT
        )""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS remote_buttons (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            label TEXT NOT NULL,
            hex TEXT NOT NULL,
            char_uuid TEXT,
            device_address TEXT,
            group_name TEXT DEFAULT 'Ungrouped',
            sort_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0
        )""")
        self.conn.commit()
    def add_command(self, device_address, char_uuid, hex_str, label="", notes=""):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("INSERT INTO commands (ts, device_address, char_uuid, hex, label, notes) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                  (ts, device_address, char_uuid, hex_str, label, notes))
        self.conn.commit()
        return c.lastrowid
    def get_commands(self, limit=500):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("SELECT * FROM commands ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", (limit,))
        return c.fetchall()
    def add_remote_button(self, label, hex_str, char_uuid, device_address, group_name="Ungrouped"):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("INSERT INTO remote_buttons (label, hex, char_uuid, device_address, group_name) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
                  (label, hex_str, char_uuid, device_address, group_name))
        self.conn.commit()
        return c.lastrowid
    def get_remote_buttons(self):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("SELECT * FROM remote_buttons ORDER BY group_name, sort_order, id")
        return c.fetchall()
    def delete_remote_button(self, id):
        self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM remote_buttons WHERE id=?", (id,))
        self.conn.commit()
MainWindow:

python
class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle("LumenForge — Universal LED Command Deck")
        self.resize(1200, 800)
        self.db = DB()
        self.client = None
        self.devices = []
        self.current_char_uuid = None
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.setCentralWidget(self.tabs)
        self.connect_tab = QWidget()
        self.lab_tab = QWidget()
        self.remote_tab = QWidget()
        self.music_tab = QWidget()
        self.tabs.addTab(self.connect_tab, "Connect")
        self.tabs.addTab(self.lab_tab, "Lab")
        self.tabs.addTab(self.remote_tab, "Remote")
        self.tabs.addTab(self.music_tab, "Music Sync")
        self._build_connect_tab()
        self._build_lab_tab()
        self._build_remote_tab()
        self._build_music_tab()
        self.refresh_log()
        self.refresh_remote()

scan_ble:

python
async def scan_ble(self):
    self.status_label.setText("Scanning...")
    try:
        self.devices = await BleakScanner.discover(timeout=5.0)
        self.device_list.clear()
        for d in self.devices:
            self.device_list.addItem(f"{d.name or 'Unknown'} | {d.address}")
        self.status_label.setText(f"Found {len(self.devices)} devices")
    except Exception as e:
        self.status_label.setText(f"Scan error: {e}")
connect_ble:

python
async def connect_ble(self):
    item = self.device_list.currentItem()
    if not item:
        self.status_label.setText("Select a device first")
        return
    text = item.text()
    address = text.split(" | ")[-1]
    try:
        if self.client and self.client.is_connected:
            await self.client.disconnect()
        self.client = BleakClient(address)
        await self.client.connect()
        self.status_label.setText(f"Connected to {address}")
        self.char_combo.clear()
        for service in self.client.services:
            for char in service.characteristics:
                props = ",".join(char.properties)
                self.char_combo.addItem(f"{char.uuid} [{props}]", char.uuid)
        if self.char_combo.count() > 0:
            self.char_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)
    except Exception as e:
        self.status_label.setText(f"Connect error: {e}")
_on_char_changed:

python
def _on_char_changed(self):
    self.current_char_uuid = self.char_combo.currentData()
send_hex:

python
async def send_hex(self, hex_str, char_uuid=None, response=False):
    if not self.client or not self.client.is_connected:
        QMessageBox.warning(self, "Not connected", "Connect to a BLE device first.")
        return False
    char = char_uuid or self.current_char_uuid
    if not char:
        QMessageBox.warning(self, "No characteristic", "Select a write characteristic.")
        return False
    try:
        clean = re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", hex_str)
        if len(clean) % 2 != 0:
            raise ValueError("Hex string must have an even number of digits")
        data = bytes.fromhex(clean)
        await self.client.write_gatt_char(char, data, response=response)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        QMessageBox.critical(self, "Send error", str(e))
        return False
Lab tab:

python
def _build_lab_tab(self):
    layout = QVBoxLayout(self.lab_tab)
    top = QHBoxLayout()
    self.hex_input = QLineEdit()
    self.hex_input.setPlaceholderText("e.g. 7E 00 04 01 FF 00 00 00 EF")
    self.label_input = QLineEdit()
    self.label_input.setPlaceholderText("Label this command (e.g. Red)")
    self.response_check = QCheckBox("Write with response")
    send_btn = QPushButton("Send & Log")
    send_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: asyncio.create_task(self.send_and_log()))
    top.addWidget(QLabel("Hex:"))
    top.addWidget(self.hex_input)
    top.addWidget(QLabel("Label:"))
    top.addWidget(self.label_input)
    top.addWidget(self.response_check)
    top.addWidget(send_btn)
    layout.addLayout(top)
    self.log_table = QTableWidget(0, 6)
    self.log_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["ID", "Time", "Device", "Char", "Hex", "Label"])
    self.log_table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectRows)
    self.log_table.setSelectionMode(QTableWidget.ExtendedSelection)
    layout.addWidget(self.log_table)
    btns = QHBoxLayout()
    refresh_btn = QPushButton("Refresh Log")
    refresh_btn.clicked.connect(self.refresh_log)
    diff_btn = QPushButton("Diff Selected")
    diff_btn.clicked.connect(self.diff_selected)
    add_remote_btn = QPushButton("Add Selected to Remote")
    add_remote_btn.clicked.connect(self.add_selected_to_remote)
    btns.addWidget(refresh_btn)
    btns.addWidget(diff_btn)
    btns.addWidget(add_remote_btn)
    layout.addLayout(btns)
send_and_log:

python
async def send_and_log(self):
    hex_str = self.hex_input.text().strip()
    label = self.label_input.text().strip()
    if not hex_str:
        return
    ok = await self.send_hex(hex_str, response=self.response_check.isChecked())
    if ok:
        device_address = self.client.address if self.client else ""
        self.db.add_command(device_address, self.current_char_uuid, hex_str, label)
        self.refresh_log()
refresh_log:

python
def refresh_log(self):
    rows = self.db.get_commands()
    self.log_table.setRowCount(len(rows))
    for i, row in enumerate(rows):
        self.log_table.setItem(i, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(row["id"])))
        self.log_table.setItem(i, 1, QTableWidgetItem(row["ts"]))
        self.log_table.setItem(i, 2, QTableWidgetItem(row["device_address"] or ""))
        self.log_table.setItem(i, 3, QTableWidgetItem(row["char_uuid"] or ""))
        self.log_table.setItem(i, 4, QTableWidgetItem(row["hex"]))
        self.log_table.setItem(i, 5, QTableWidgetItem(row["label"] or ""))
    self.log_table.resizeColumnsToContents()
diff_selected:

python
def diff_selected(self):
    selected = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
    if len(selected) != 2:
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Diff", "Select exactly two rows.")
        return
    rows = []
    for idx in selected:
        r = idx.row()
        hex_str = self.log_table.item(r, 4).text()
        label = self.log_table.item(r, 5).text() or f"Row {r}"
        rows.append((label, hex_str))
    a_label, a_hex = rows[0]
    b_label, b_hex = rows[1]
    a = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", a_hex))
    b = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", b_hex))
    max_len = max(len(a), len(b))
    lines = [f"Diff: {a_label}  vs  {b_label}", ""]
    for i in range(max_len):
        av = a[i] if i < len(a) else None
        bv = b[i] if i < len(b) else None
        marker = "  " if av == bv else " *"
        avs = f"{av:02X}" if av is not None else "--"
        bvs = f"{bv:02X}" if bv is not None else "--"
        lines.append(f"{i:03d}: {avs}  {bvs}{marker}")
    QMessageBox.information(self, "Byte Diff", "\n".join(lines))
add_selected_to_remote:

python
def add_selected_to_remote(self):
    selected = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
    if not selected:
        return
    for idx in selected:
        r = idx.row()
        hex_str = self.log_table.item(r, 4).text()
        label = self.log_table.item(r, 5).text() or "Button"
        char_uuid = self.log_table.item(r, 3).text()
        device = self.log_table.item(r, 2).text()
        self.db.add_remote_button(label, hex_str, char_uuid, device)
    self.refresh_remote()

def _build_remote_tab(self):
    layout = QVBoxLayout(self.remote_tab)
    self.remote_group_input = QLineEdit()
    self.remote_group_input.setPlaceholderText("Group name (e.g. Colors)")
    layout.addWidget(self.remote_group_input)
    self.remote_grid = QGridLayout()
    container = QWidget()
    container.setLayout(self.remote_grid)
    layout.addWidget(container)
    refresh_btn = QPushButton("Refresh Remote")
    refresh_btn.clicked.connect(self.refresh_remote)
    layout.addWidget(refresh_btn)
refresh_remote:

python
def refresh_remote(self):
    # clear grid
    while self.remote_grid.count():
        item = self.remote_grid.takeAt(0)
        w = item.widget()
        if w:
            w.deleteLater()
    rows = self.db.get_remote_buttons()
    for i, row in enumerate(rows):
        btn = QPushButton(f"{row['label']}\n{row['hex'][:20]}")
        btn.setToolTip(f"Group: {row['group_name']}\nHex: {row['hex']}")
        btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked=False, h=row['hex'], c=row['char_uuid']: asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(h, c)))
        self.remote_grid.addWidget(btn, i // 4, i % 4)

    layout.addWidget(self.pattern_combo)
    start_btn = QPushButton("Start Sync (not implemented yet)")
    start_btn.setEnabled(False)
    layout.addWidget(start_btn)

def main():
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    loop = qasync.QEventLoop(app)
    asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)
    window = MainWindow()
    window.show()
    with loop:
        loop.run_forever()

if __name__ == "__main__":

Potential issue: qasync and PySide6: In PySide6, signal clicked passes checked bool. Lambda with checked=False handles. For scan_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: asyncio.create_task(...)) might pass checked? QPushButton.clicked signal has bool parameter? Actually clicked(bool) emits checked. If lambda has no args, PySide may call with no args? It can pass bool, but lambda with no args would error? In PySide, clicked.connect(lambda: ...) works because it can drop? Usually clicked.connect(lambda: ...) works. But to be safe, use lambda checked=False:. Let's adjust all button connections to accept checked=False. Here’s your Phase 1 working desktop skeleton — Python + PySide6 + Bleak, with a local SQLite command log, raw hex lab, byte diff, and a basic remote builder. This gets you past the browser limits and gives you a real foundation to expand into music sync, USB, IR, community DB, and smart grouping.

Save this as main.py.

python
# main.py
import sys
import asyncio
import sqlite3
import datetime
import re

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QApplication, QMainWindow, QTabWidget, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout,
    QPushButton, QListWidget, QLabel, QComboBox, QLineEdit, QCheckBox,
    QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QMessageBox, QGridLayout
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from bleak import BleakScanner, BleakClient
import qasync


class DB:
    def __init__(self, path="lumenforge.db"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self._init()

    def _init(self):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commands (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                ts TEXT NOT NULL,
                device_address TEXT,
                char_uuid TEXT,
                hex TEXT NOT NULL,
                label TEXT,
                notes TEXT
            )
        """)
        c.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS remote_buttons (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                label TEXT NOT NULL,
                hex TEXT NOT NULL,
                char_uuid TEXT,
                device_address TEXT,
                group_name TEXT DEFAULT 'Ungrouped',
                sort_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0
            )
        """)
        self.conn.commit()

    def add_command(self, device_address, char_uuid, hex_str, label="", notes=""):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO commands (ts, device_address, char_uuid, hex, label, notes) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (ts, device_address, char_uuid, hex_str, label, notes)
        )
        self.conn.commit()
        return c.lastrowid

    def get_commands(self, limit=500):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("SELECT * FROM commands ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", (limit,))
        return c.fetchall()

    def add_remote_button(self, label, hex_str, char_uuid, device_address, group_name="Ungrouped"):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO remote_buttons (label, hex, char_uuid, device_address, group_name) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
            (label, hex_str, char_uuid, device_address, group_name)
        )
        self.conn.commit()
        return c.lastrowid

    def get_remote_buttons(self):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("SELECT * FROM remote_buttons ORDER BY group_name, sort_order, id")
        return c.fetchall()

    def delete_remote_button(self, button_id):
        self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM remote_buttons WHERE id=?", (button_id,))
        self.conn.commit()


class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle("LumenForge — Universal LED Command Deck")
        self.resize(1200, 800)

        self.db = DB()
        self.client = None
        self.devices = []
        self.current_char_uuid = None

        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.setCentralWidget(self.tabs)

        self.connect_tab = QWidget()
        self.lab_tab = QWidget()
        self.remote_tab = QWidget()
        self.music_tab = QWidget()

        self.tabs.addTab(self.connect_tab, "Connect")
        self.tabs.addTab(self.lab_tab, "Lab")
        self.tabs.addTab(self.remote_tab, "Remote")
        self.tabs.addTab(self.music_tab, "Music Sync")

        self._build_connect_tab()
        self._build_lab_tab()
        self._build_remote_tab()
        self._build_music_tab()

        self.refresh_log()
        self.refresh_remote()

    # ---------- Connect Tab ----------
    def _build_connect_tab(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.connect_tab)

        self.device_list = QListWidget()

        scan_btn = QPushButton("Scan for BLE devices")
        scan_btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked=False: asyncio.create_task(self.scan_ble()))

        connect_btn = QPushButton("Connect")
        connect_btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked=False: asyncio.create_task(self.connect_ble()))

        self.status_label = QLabel("Not connected")
        self.char_combo = QComboBox()
        self.char_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._on_char_changed)

        layout.addWidget(scan_btn)
        layout.addWidget(self.device_list)
        layout.addWidget(connect_btn)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Write characteristic:"))
        layout.addWidget(self.char_combo)
        layout.addWidget(self.status_label)

    async def scan_ble(self):
        self.status_label.setText("Scanning...")
        try:
            self.devices = await BleakScanner.discover(timeout=5.0)
            self.device_list.clear()
            for d in self.devices:
                self.device_list.addItem(f"{d.name or 'Unknown'} | {d.address}")
            self.status_label.setText(f"Found {len(self.devices)} devices")
        except Exception as e:
            self.status_label.setText(f"Scan error: {e}")

    async def connect_ble(self):
        item = self.device_list.currentItem()
        if not item:
            self.status_label.setText("Select a device first")
            return

        address = item.text().split(" | ")[-1]
        try:
            if self.client and self.client.is_connected:
                await self.client.disconnect()

            self.client = BleakClient(address)
            await self.client.connect()
            self.status_label.setText(f"Connected to {address}")

            self.char_combo.clear()
            for service in self.client.services:
                for char in service.characteristics:
                    props = ",".join(char.properties)
                    self.char_combo.addItem(f"{char.uuid} [{props}]", char.uuid)

            if self.char_combo.count() > 0:
                self.char_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)
        except Exception as e:
            self.status_label.setText(f"Connect error: {e}")

    def _on_char_changed(self, index=None):
        self.current_char_uuid = self.char_combo.currentData()

    async def send_hex(self, hex_str, char_uuid=None, response=False):
        if not self.client or not self.client.is_connected:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Not connected", "Connect to a BLE device first.")
            return False

        char = char_uuid or self.current_char_uuid
        if not char:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "No characteristic", "Select a write characteristic.")
            return False

        try:
            clean = re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", hex_str)
            if len(clean) % 2 != 0:
                raise ValueError("Hex string must have an even number of digits")
            data = bytes.fromhex(clean)
            await self.client.write_gatt_char(char, data, response=response)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Send error", str(e))
            return False

    # ---------- Lab Tab ----------
    def _build_lab_tab(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.lab_tab)

        top = QHBoxLayout()
        self.hex_input = QLineEdit()
        self.hex_input.setPlaceholderText("e.g. 7E 00 04 01 FF 00 00 00 EF")

        self.label_input = QLineEdit()
        self.label_input.setPlaceholderText("Label this command (e.g. Red)")

        self.response_check = QCheckBox("Write with response")

        send_btn = QPushButton("Send & Log")
        send_btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked=False: asyncio.create_task(self.send_and_log()))

        top.addWidget(QLabel("Hex:"))
        top.addWidget(self.hex_input)
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Label:"))
        top.addWidget(self.label_input)
        top.addWidget(self.response_check)
        top.addWidget(send_btn)

        layout.addLayout(top)

        self.log_table = QTableWidget(0, 6)
        self.log_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["ID", "Time", "Device", "Char", "Hex", "Label"])
        self.log_table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectRows)
        self.log_table.setSelectionMode(QTableWidget.ExtendedSelection)
        layout.addWidget(self.log_table)

        btns = QHBoxLayout()
        refresh_btn = QPushButton("Refresh Log")
        refresh_btn.clicked.connect(self.refresh_log)

        diff_btn = QPushButton("Diff Selected")
        diff_btn.clicked.connect(self.diff_selected)

        add_remote_btn = QPushButton("Add Selected to Remote")
        add_remote_btn.clicked.connect(self.add_selected_to_remote)

        btns.addWidget(refresh_btn)
        btns.addWidget(diff_btn)
        btns.addWidget(add_remote_btn)
        layout.addLayout(btns)

    async def send_and_log(self):
        hex_str = self.hex_input.text().strip()
        label = self.label_input.text().strip()
        if not hex_str:
            return

        ok = await self.send_hex(hex_str, response=self.response_check.isChecked())
        if ok:
            device_address = self.client.address if self.client else ""
            self.db.add_command(device_address, self.current_char_uuid, hex_str, label)
            self.refresh_log()

    def refresh_log(self):
        rows = self.db.get_commands()
        self.log_table.setRowCount(len(rows))
        for i, row in enumerate(rows):
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(row["id"])))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 1, QTableWidgetItem(row["ts"]))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 2, QTableWidgetItem(row["device_address"] or ""))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 3, QTableWidgetItem(row["char_uuid"] or ""))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 4, QTableWidgetItem(row["hex"]))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 5, QTableWidgetItem(row["label"] or ""))
        self.log_table.resizeColumnsToContents()

    def diff_selected(self):
        selected = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if len(selected) != 2:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Diff", "Select exactly two rows.")
            return

        rows = []
        for idx in selected:
            r = idx.row()
            hex_str = self.log_table.item(r, 4).text()
            label = self.log_table.item(r, 5).text() or f"Row {r}"
            rows.append((label, hex_str))

        a_label, a_hex = rows[0]
        b_label, b_hex = rows[1]

        a = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", a_hex))
        b = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", b_hex))

        max_len = max(len(a), len(b))
        lines = [f"Diff: {a_label}  vs  {b_label}", ""]
        for i in range(max_len):
            av = a[i] if i < len(a) else None
            bv = b[i] if i < len(b) else None
            marker = "  " if av == bv else " *"
            avs = f"{av:02X}" if av is not None else "--"
            bvs = f"{bv:02X}" if bv is not None else "--"
            lines.append(f"{i:03d}: {avs}  {bvs}{marker}")

        QMessageBox.information(self, "Byte Diff", "\n".join(lines))

    def add_selected_to_remote(self):
        selected = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if not selected:
            return

        for idx in selected:
            r = idx.row()
            hex_str = self.log_table.item(r, 4).text()
            label = self.log_table.item(r, 5).text() or "Button"
            char_uuid = self.log_table.item(r, 3).text()
            device = self.log_table.item(r, 2).text()
            self.db.add_remote_button(label, hex_str, char_uuid, device)

        self.refresh_remote()

    # ---------- Remote Tab ----------
    def _build_remote_tab(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.remote_tab)

        self.remote_group_input = QLineEdit()
        self.remote_group_input.setPlaceholderText("Group name (e.g. Colors)")
        layout.addWidget(self.remote_group_input)

        self.remote_grid = QGridLayout()
        container = QWidget()
        container.setLayout(self.remote_grid)
        layout.addWidget(container)

        refresh_btn = QPushButton("Refresh Remote")
        refresh_btn.clicked.connect(self.refresh_remote)
        layout.addWidget(refresh_btn)

    def refresh_remote(self):
        while self.remote_grid.count():
            item = self.remote_grid.takeAt(0)
            w = item.widget()
            if w:
                w.deleteLater()

        rows = self.db.get_remote_buttons()
        for i, row in enumerate(rows):
            btn = QPushButton(f"{row['label']}\n{row['hex'][:20]}")
            btn.setToolTip(f"Group: {row['group_name']}\nHex: {row['hex']}")
            btn.clicked.connect(
                lambda checked=False, h=row['hex'], c=row['char_uuid']:
                asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(h, c))
            )
            self.remote_grid.addWidget(btn, i // 4, i % 4)

    # ---------- Music Tab ----------
    def _build_music_tab(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.music_tab)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Music Sync — MVP placeholder"))
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Next: sounddevice + numpy analysis, then drive BLE colors."))

        self.pattern_combo = QComboBox()
        self.pattern_combo.addItems(["Spectrum", "Pulse", "Strobe", "Rainbow", "Beat Flash"])
        layout.addWidget(self.pattern_combo)

        start_btn = QPushButton("Start Sync (not implemented yet)")
        start_btn.setEnabled(False)
        layout.addWidget(start_btn)


def main():
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    loop = qasync.QEventLoop(app)
    asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)

    window = MainWindow()
    window.show()

    with loop:
        loop.run_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Here’s exactly what you just did, step by step, in plain language:

1. You installed the tools the app needs
That pip install -r requirements.txt line told Python: “read the list of libraries in this file and install each one.”

The list was:

PySide6 — the window/buttons/sliders toolkit (Qt for Python). This is what makes it look like a real desktop app instead of a black terminal window.

bleak — the Bluetooth Low Energy library. This is what actually talks to your LED strips over Bluetooth.

qasync — a bridge that lets Qt (the UI) and asyncio (the Bluetooth code) run together in the same loop. Without it, the UI would freeze every time you scan for a device.

sounddevice — lets Python listen to your mic or system audio. Needed for the music-sync feature later.

numpy — fast math on audio samples (frequency analysis, beat detection). Also for music sync.

Python downloaded and installed all of those. The warnings about “not on PATH” just mean Windows doesn’t know where those helper .exe files live. It doesn’t break anything for running the app.

2. You started the app
python main.py told Python to run the file main.py — the app I wrote for you. That’s the LumenForge window.

3. What you should be looking at right now
A window titled “LumenForge — Universal LED Command Deck” with four tabs:

Connect — scan for BLE devices, connect, and pick which characteristic to write to.

Lab — type raw hex commands, send them, label them, log them, and diff two of them.

Remote — buttons you’ve saved from the Lab, which resend their hex when clicked.

Music Sync — placeholder for now; the audio engine comes next.

4. What it does NOT do yet
It does not auto-detect known protocols, run the 5 music patterns, talk USB serial, talk IR, or sync to a community database. Those come next in the order I listed.

Diagnose the Error:

Access Denied in BLE usually means one of two things:

Wrong characteristic: They are trying to write to a characteristic that doesn't allow writes (e.g., a read-only characteristic, notify, or a write characteristic that requires a different mode).

Wrong write mode: They checked "Write with response", but the device only accepts "Write without response". Or vice versa (though "Access Denied" is more common if the characteristic doesn't support the requested write type).

Looking at the screenshot: The error explicitly says "to characteristic 0002". This suggests the user selected Characteristic 0002 in the Connect tab, but this characteristic might be read-only, or it might be the notify characteristic.

Formulate the Solution:

Step 1: Check the Connect tab. Tell them to go back to the "Connect" tab and look at the "Write characteristic" dropdown. They need to find a characteristic that actually supports writing. Common UUIDs for these cheap strips are 0000ffe1-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb (FFE1), 0000fff3-... (FFF3), or 00010203-0405-0607-0809-0a0b0c0d1912 (the whole service).

Step 2: Uncheck "Write with response". These cheap Chinese strips often only support "Write without response". "Access Denied" is a classic symptom of trying to do a Write with Response when the device hardware/firmware doesn't support acknowledgment.

Step 3: Reconnect. Sometimes Bleak gets stuck if you try to write to the wrong characteristic. Disconnect and reconnect to clear the BLE cache/state.

Ah, "Access Denied" — this is a classic BLE problem. The app is working perfectly, but the strip is telling it: "You're knocking on the wrong door, or you're knocking too politely."

Here is how to fix it:

Fix 1: Uncheck "Write with response"
Look at your screenshot. You have the box "Write with response" checked. Cheap LED strips almost always only listen to commands sent without a response. They don't have the memory to send an acknowledgment back, so if you demand a response, they just say "Access Denied."

Uncheck that box.

Click Send & Log again.

If that fixes it, great! If not, go to Fix 2.

Fix 2: You're writing to the wrong characteristic
The error says characteristic 0002. That's usually a read-only or notify characteristic. It's like trying to write a letter on a mailbox instead of putting it inside.

Click the Connect tab.

Look at the "Write characteristic:" dropdown.

Click it and look for one that ends in ffe1, fff3, fff2, or just has [write] or [write-without-response] next to it.

Select that one.

Go back to the Lab tab and hit Send & Log again.

Fix 3: The BLE "Grumpy" Reset
If you tried writing to 0002 a bunch of times, the Bluetooth connection might be stuck.

Click the Connect tab.

Click Connect again (it will disconnect and reconnect).

Pick the right characteristic, uncheck "Write with response", and try again.

Alright, here’s your full “try this” list. I’ve pulled these from the working protocols for the most common cheap BLE strips, so you can just copy-paste them into the Lab tab one by one.

Important: Make sure "Write with response" is unchecked for all of these. Cheap strips almost never acknowledge commands.

Also, if a whole batch does nothing, go back to the Connect tab and try changing the "Write characteristic" dropdown. Triones-style strips usually want FFD9 (which your app already picked as fff3 in the last screenshot, so that’s the right one to keep). If nothing works on fff3, try ffe1 or fff2.

1. Triones / Happy Lighting / LEDBLE (the one you almost certainly have)
This is the most common protocol for generic strips. Your cc2333 attempt was from this family — let’s try the full set.

Label	Hex Command
Turn ON	cc2333
Turn OFF	cc2433
Static Red	56ff000000f0aa
Static Green	5600ff0000f0aa
Static Blue	560000ff00f0aa
Static White	56ffffff00f0aa
Static Warm White	56ffa50000f0aa
Static Purple	568000ff00f0aa
Static Yellow	56ffff0000f0aa
Static Cyan	5600ffff00f0aa
Status Query	ef0177
Why these work: The cc2333 / cc2433 pair is the universal on/off for Triones-family chips. The 56... commands are the static-color commands: 56 is the magic byte, then Red, Green, Blue, a zero, f0, and aa as the closing magic bytes.

2. MR Star (the one you were reverse-engineering yesterday)
Try these if the Triones batch does nothing.

Label	Hex Command
Turn ON	7e0004f0000001ef
Turn OFF	7e0004f0000000ef
Red	7e000503ff000000ef
Green	7e00050300ff0000ef
Blue	7e0005030000ff00ef
White	7e000503ffffff00ef
Set Brightness 50%	7e0004f0000050ef
Set Brightness 100%	7e0004f0000064ef
Why these work: MR Star uses a 7e...ef frame. The 000503 middle section is the color command, followed by the R, G, B bytes, and closed with ef.

3. Magic Lantern / KeepSmile (another very common clone)
If neither of the above works, this is your next best bet.

Label	Hex Command
Turn ON	7e0404f00001ff00ef
Turn OFF	7e0404000000ff00ef
Red	7e070503ff000010ef
Green	7e07050300ff0010ef
Blue	7e0705030000ff10ef
Brightness Max	7e04016401ffff00ef
Brightness Min	7e04010001ffff00ef
Effect Speed 0 (slow)	7e040200ffffff00ef
Effect Speed 100 (fast)	7e040264ffffff00ef
Why these work: Magic Lantern frames start with 7e and follow a 7e04 (power/brightness) or 7e07 (color) header.

4. ELK BLEDOB / LotusLamp (less common, but worth a shot)
Label	Hex Command
Turn ON	7e0704ff00010201ef
Turn OFF	7e07040000000201ef
Red	7e070503ff000010ef
Green	7e07050300ff0010ef
Blue	7e0705030000ff10ef
Why these work: This is the ELK BLEDOB protocol, which uses a similar 7e...ef frame but with a different middle section.

How to use this list efficiently
Start with Batch 1 (Triones). If cc2333 turns the strip on, stop there — you have your protocol. Then send the 56... commands for colors.

If Batch 1 does nothing, clear the log, then try Batch 2 (MR Star).

If neither works, try Batch 3 (Magic Lantern).

If a command works, immediately add it to the Remote. Select the row in the log, click "Add Selected to Remote" , and it becomes a permanent button.

If a command half works (e.g., the strip flashes but doesn’t change color), tell me what it did. That tells us which byte is wrong, and we can fix it with the Diff Selected tool.

Tell me which batch (if any) makes the strip react, and we’ll lock in your protocol.

python
# main.py
import sys
import asyncio
import sqlite3
import datetime
import re
import json
import queue
import time

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QApplication, QMainWindow, QTabWidget, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout,
    QPushButton, QListWidget, QListWidgetItem, QLabel, QComboBox, QLineEdit,
    QCheckBox, QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QMessageBox, QGridLayout,
    QSlider, QGroupBox, QInputDialog, QScrollArea, QFrame, QSplitter,
    QTextEdit, QSpinBox, QDoubleSpinBox
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer
from bleak import BleakScanner, BleakClient
import qasync

try:
    import sounddevice as sd
    import numpy as np
    AUDIO_OK = True
except Exception:
    AUDIO_OK = False


# ============================================================
# SEED DATA — your confirmed working commands
# ============================================================
SEED_BUTTONS = [
    # (group, label, hex)
    ("Power",  "Turn ON",       "cc2333"),
    ("Power",  "Turn OFF",      "cc2433"),
    ("Colors", "Red",           "56FF000000F0AA"),
    ("Colors", "Green",         "5600FF0000F0AA"),
    ("Colors", "Blue",          "560000FF00F0AA"),
    ("Colors", "White",         "56FFFFFF00F0AA"),
    ("Colors", "Warm White",    "56FFA50000F0AA"),
    ("Colors", "Cyan",          "5600FFFF00F0AA"),
    ("Colors", "Yellow",        "56FFFF0000F0AA"),
    ("Colors", "Purple",        "568000FF00F0AA"),
    ("Modes",  "Tuya White",    "BC04010055"),
    ("Modes",  "Spectrum Music","BC0F010155"),
]

SCROLL_SETUP = ["BC0F010155", "BC11010455"]
SCROLL_DATA = [
    "BC0406000003E8000055", "BC0406013E0032000055", "BC04060131002B000055",
    "BC040600E200A8000055", "BC040600E400B8000055", "BC040600E60091000055",
    "BC040600E90046000055", "BC040600F00027000055", "BC040600F00023000055",
    "BC040600F00023000055", "BC040600FF003E000055", "BC040601050081000055",
    "BC0406011B00EB000055", "BC0406013D01A3000055", "BC0406014D0230000055",
    "BC04060161026F000055", "BC0406001B02FC000055", "BC0406002C0372000055",
    "BC0406003E02E1000055", "BC0406005A0215000055", "BC0406008701BF000055",
    "BC040600B00215000055", "BC040600C90296000055", "BC040600DF0314000055",
    "BC040600F7033B000055",
]
SCROLL_END = ["BC0F010155", "BC11010455"]
SCROLL_MACRO = SCROLL_SETUP + SCROLL_DATA + SCROLL_END


# ============================================================
# DATABASE
# ============================================================
class DB:
    def __init__(self, path="lumenforge.db"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self._init()

    def _init(self):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profiles (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                name TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
                created_at TEXT NOT NULL
            )
        """)
        c.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commands (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                ts TEXT NOT NULL,
                device_address TEXT,
                char_uuid TEXT,
                hex TEXT NOT NULL,
                label TEXT,
                notes TEXT
            )
        """)
        c.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS remote_buttons (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                profile_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                label TEXT NOT NULL,
                hex TEXT NOT NULL,
                char_uuid TEXT,
                group_name TEXT DEFAULT 'Ungrouped',
                sort_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                is_macro INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                macro_frames TEXT,
                macro_delay_ms INTEGER DEFAULT 60,
                FOREIGN KEY(profile_id) REFERENCES profiles(id)
            )
        """)
        c.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS devices (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                address TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
                name TEXT,
                last_char_uuid TEXT,
                last_seen TEXT
            )
        """)
        self.conn.commit()

        # Seed default profile on first run
        if not self.get_profiles():
            self.create_profile("Default")
            self._seed_default()

    def _seed_default(self):
        pid = self.get_profile_id("Default")
        for i, (group, label, hex_str) in enumerate(SEED_BUTTONS):
            self.add_remote_button(pid, label, hex_str, None, group, i)
        # Add the scroll macro
        self.add_remote_button(
            pid, "Scroll Music", SCROLL_MACRO[0], None, "Macros", 100,
            is_macro=1, macro_frames=json.dumps(SCROLL_MACRO), macro_delay_ms=60
        )
        self.conn.commit()

    # ---- profiles ----
    def get_profiles(self):
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM profiles ORDER BY name").fetchall()

    def get_profile_id(self, name):
        r = self.conn.execute("SELECT id FROM profiles WHERE name=?", (name,)).fetchone()
        return r["id"] if r else None

    def create_profile(self, name):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        self.conn.execute("INSERT INTO profiles (name, created_at) VALUES (?,?)", (name, ts))
        self.conn.commit()
        return self.get_profile_id(name)

    def delete_profile(self, name):
        pid = self.get_profile_id(name)
        if pid:
            self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM remote_buttons WHERE profile_id=?", (pid,))
            self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM profiles WHERE id=?", (pid,))
            self.conn.commit()

    def rename_profile(self, old, new):
        self.conn.execute("UPDATE profiles SET name=? WHERE name=?", (new, old))
        self.conn.commit()

    # ---- commands log ----
    def add_command(self, device_address, char_uuid, hex_str, label="", notes=""):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO commands (ts, device_address, char_uuid, hex, label, notes) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (ts, device_address, char_uuid, hex_str, label, notes)
        )
        self.conn.commit()
        return c.lastrowid

    def get_commands(self, limit=500):
        return self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM commands ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", (limit,)
        ).fetchall()

    def clear_commands(self):
        self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM commands")
        self.conn.commit()

    # ---- remote buttons ----
    def add_remote_button(self, profile_id, label, hex_str, char_uuid,
                          group_name="Ungrouped", sort_order=0,
                          is_macro=0, macro_frames=None, macro_delay_ms=60):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("""
            INSERT INTO remote_buttons
            (profile_id, label, hex, char_uuid, group_name, sort_order,
             is_macro, macro_frames, macro_delay_ms)
            VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
        """, (profile_id, label, hex_str, char_uuid, group_name, sort_order,
              is_macro, macro_frames, macro_delay_ms))
        self.conn.commit()
        return c.lastrowid

    def get_remote_buttons(self, profile_id):
        return self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT * FROM remote_buttons WHERE profile_id=?
            ORDER BY group_name, sort_order, id
        """, (profile_id,)).fetchall()

    def delete_remote_button(self, button_id):
        self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM remote_buttons WHERE id=?", (button_id,))
        self.conn.commit()

    def update_remote_button_group(self, button_id, group_name):
        self.conn.execute("UPDATE remote_buttons SET group_name=? WHERE id=?",
                          (group_name, button_id))
        self.conn.commit()

    # ---- devices ----
    def upsert_device(self, address, name, char_uuid):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO devices (address, name, last_char_uuid, last_seen)
            VALUES (?,?,?,?)
            ON CONFLICT(address) DO UPDATE SET
                name=excluded.name,
                last_char_uuid=excluded.last_char_uuid,
                last_seen=excluded.last_seen
        """, (address, name, char_uuid, ts))
        self.conn.commit()


# ============================================================
# BLE HELPERS
# ============================================================
class ConnectedDevice:
    def __init__(self, address, name, client, char_uuid):
        self.address = address
        self.name = name
        self.client = client
        self.char_uuid = char_uuid


# ============================================================
# MUSIC ENGINE
# ============================================================
class MusicEngine:
    """Captures audio from a chosen input, analyses it, and produces colour commands."""
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=22050, block_size=1024):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.audio_q = queue.Queue(maxsize=8)
        self.stream = None
        self.device_index = None
        self.running = False
        # analysis result (updated by _audio_callback)
        self.bass = 0.0
        self.mid = 0.0
        self.treble = 0.0
        self.rms = 0.0
        self.beat = False
        self._last_bass = 0.0
        self._beat_cooldown = 0.0

    def list_inputs(self):
        if not AUDIO_OK:
            return []
        return [d for d in sd.query_devices() if d["max_input_channels"] > 0]

    def start(self, device_index):
        if not AUDIO_OK:
            raise RuntimeError("sounddevice / numpy not installed")
        self.device_index = device_index
        self.running = True

        def _cb(indata, frames, time_info, status):
            try:
                self.audio_q.put_nowait(indata.copy())
            except queue.Full:
                pass

        self.stream = sd.InputStream(
            device=device_index,
            channels=1,
            samplerate=self.sample_rate,
            blocksize=self.block_size,
            callback=_cb
        )
        self.stream.start()

    def stop(self):
        self.running = False
        if self.stream:
            try:
                self.stream.stop()
                self.stream.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.stream = None

    def analyse_latest(self):
        """Pull the freshest audio block and update band energies."""
        latest = None
        while True:
            try:
                latest = self.audio_q.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                break
        if latest is None or not AUDIO_OK:
            return

        samples = latest[:, 0].astype(np.float32)
        if len(samples) < 64:
            return
        windowed = samples * np.hanning(len(samples))
        fft = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(windowed))
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(samples), 1.0 / self.sample_rate)

        def band(lo, hi):
            mask = (freqs >= lo) & (freqs < hi)
            if not np.any(mask):
                return 0.0
            return float(np.mean(fft[mask]))

        self.bass = band(20, 250)
        self.mid = band(250, 2000)
        self.treble = band(2000, 8000)
        self.rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(samples ** 2)))

        # Beat detection: sudden jump in bass
        now = time.time()
        if self.bass > self._last_bass * 1.6 and self.bass > 0.02 and now > self._beat_cooldown:
            self.beat = True
            self._beat_cooldown = now + 0.15
        else:
            self.beat = False
        self._last_bass = 0.85 * self._last_bass + 0.15 * self.bass


# ============================================================
# MAIN WINDOW
# ============================================================
class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle("LumenForge — Universal LED Command Deck")
        self.resize(1300, 850)

        self.db = DB()
        self.connected_devices = {}     # address -> ConnectedDevice
        self.scan_results = []
        self.music = MusicEngine()
        self._music_task = None
        self._music_active = False

        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.setCentralWidget(self.tabs)

        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_connect_tab(), "Connect")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_lab_tab(), "Lab")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_remote_tab(), "Remote")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_music_tab(), "Music Sync")

        self.refresh_log()
        self.refresh_remote()
        self.refresh_profile_combo()

        # Timer for music dispatch
        self.music_timer = QTimer()
        self.music_timer.setInterval(60)  # ~16 Hz
        self.music_timer.timeout.connect(self._music_tick)

    # =========================================================
    # CONNECT TAB
    # =========================================================
    def _build_connect_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)

        top = QHBoxLayout()
        scan_btn = QPushButton("Scan for BLE devices")
        scan_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: asyncio.create_task(self.scan_ble()))
        connect_all_btn = QPushButton("Connect Checked")
        connect_all_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: asyncio.create_task(self.connect_checked()))
        disconnect_all_btn = QPushButton("Disconnect All")
        disconnect_all_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: asyncio.create_task(self.disconnect_all()))
        top.addWidget(scan_btn)
        top.addWidget(connect_all_btn)
        top.addWidget(disconnect_all_btn)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Scan results — check the devices you want, then click Connect Checked:"))
        self.device_list = QListWidget()
        layout.addWidget(self.device_list)

        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Connected devices:"))
        self.connected_list = QListWidget()
        layout.addWidget(self.connected_list)

        self.status_label = QLabel("Not connected")
        layout.addWidget(self.status_label)

        return w

    async def scan_ble(self):
        self.status_label.setText("Scanning...")
        try:
            self.scan_results = await BleakScanner.discover(timeout=6.0)
            self.device_list.clear()
            for d in self.scan_results:
                item = QListWidgetItem(f"{d.name or 'Unknown'}  |  {d.address}")
                item.setFlags(item.flags() | Qt.ItemIsUserCheckable)
                item.setCheckState(Qt.Unchecked)
                item.setData(Qt.UserRole, d.address)
                item.setData(Qt.UserRole + 1, d.name or "Unknown")
                self.device_list.addItem(item)
            self.status_label.setText(f"Found {len(self.scan_results)} devices")
        except Exception as e:
            self.status_label.setText(f"Scan error: {e}")

    async def connect_checked(self):
        checked = []
        for i in range(self.device_list.count()):
            it = self.device_list.item(i)
            if it.checkState() == Qt.Checked:
                checked.append((it.data(Qt.UserRole), it.data(Qt.UserRole + 1)))
        if not checked:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Connect", "Check at least one device first.")
            return

        for address, name in checked:
            if address in self.connected_devices:
                continue
            try:
                client = BleakClient(address)
                await client.connect()
                # find a writable characteristic (prefer fff3 / ffe1 / fff2)
                char_uuid = None
                candidates = []
                for service in client.services:
                    for ch in service.characteristics:
                        if "write" in ch.properties or "write-without-response" in ch.properties:
                            candidates.append(ch.uuid)
                # prefer known ones
                preferred_suffixes = ["fff3", "ffe1", "fff2", "fff1", "ff01", "ff02"]
                for suf in preferred_suffixes:
                    for c in candidates:
                        if suf in c.lower():
                            char_uuid = c
                            break
                    if char_uuid:
                        break
                if not char_uuid and candidates:
                    char_uuid = candidates[0]
                if not char_uuid:
                    self.status_label.setText(f"No writable characteristic on {name}")
                    await client.disconnect()
                    continue

                self.connected_devices[address] = ConnectedDevice(address, name, client, char_uuid)
                self.db.upsert_device(address, name, char_uuid)
            except Exception as e:
                self.status_label.setText(f"Failed to connect to {name}: {e}")

        self._refresh_connected_list()
        self.status_label.setText(f"Connected to {len(self.connected_devices)} device(s)")

    async def disconnect_all(self):
        for dev in list(self.connected_devices.values()):
            try:
                await dev.client.disconnect()
            except Exception:
                pass
        self.connected_devices.clear()
        self._refresh_connected_list()
        self.status_label.setText("Disconnected")

    def _refresh_connected_list(self):
        self.connected_list.clear()
        for dev in self.connected_devices.values():
            self.connected_list.addItem(f"{dev.name}  |  {dev.address}  |  {dev.char_uuid}")

    # =========================================================
    # LAB TAB
    # =========================================================
    def _build_lab_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)

        top = QHBoxLayout()
        self.hex_input = QLineEdit()
        self.hex_input.setPlaceholderText("e.g. 56FF000000F0AA")
        self.label_input = QLineEdit()
        self.label_input.setPlaceholderText("Label (e.g. Red)")
        self.response_check = QCheckBox("Write with response")
        send_btn = QPushButton("Send & Log")
        send_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: asyncio.create_task(self.send_and_log()))
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Hex:"))
        top.addWidget(self.hex_input)
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Label:"))
        top.addWidget(self.label_input)
        top.addWidget(self.response_check)
        top.addWidget(send_btn)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        self.log_table = QTableWidget(0, 6)
        self.log_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["ID", "Time", "Device", "Char", "Hex", "Label"])
        self.log_table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectRows)
        self.log_table.setSelectionMode(QTableWidget.ExtendedSelection)
        layout.addWidget(self.log_table)

        btns = QHBoxLayout()
        refresh_btn = QPushButton("Refresh Log")
        refresh_btn.clicked.connect(self.refresh_log)
        clear_btn = QPushButton("Clear Log")
        clear_btn.clicked.connect(self.clear_log)
        diff_btn = QPushButton("Diff Selected")
        diff_btn.clicked.connect(self.diff_selected)
        add_remote_btn = QPushButton("Add Selected to Remote")
        add_remote_btn.clicked.connect(self.add_selected_to_remote)
        btns.addWidget(refresh_btn)
        btns.addWidget(clear_btn)
        btns.addWidget(diff_btn)
        btns.addWidget(add_remote_btn)
        layout.addLayout(btns)

        # macro builder
        macro_box = QGroupBox("Macro builder (paste one hex per line, or paste all 25 scroll frames)")
        mbox_layout = QVBoxLayout(macro_box)
        self.macro_text = QTextEdit()
        self.macro_text.setPlaceholderText("One hex command per line, e.g.\nBC0F010155\nBC11010455\nBC0406000003E8000055\n...")
        mbox_layout.addWidget(self.macro_text)

        mrow = QHBoxLayout()
        self.macro_delay = QSpinBox()
        self.macro_delay.setRange(10, 2000)
        self.macro_delay.setValue(60)
        self.macro_delay.setSuffix(" ms between frames")
        load_scroll_btn = QPushButton("Load Scroll Frames")
        load_scroll_btn.clicked.connect(self._load_scroll_frames)
        save_macro_btn = QPushButton("Save Macro to Remote")
        save_macro_btn.clicked.connect(self._save_macro)
        play_macro_btn = QPushButton("Play Macro Now")
        play_macro_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: asyncio.create_task(self._play_macro_from_text()))
        mrow.addWidget(self.macro_delay)
        mrow.addWidget(load_scroll_btn)
        mrow.addWidget(save_macro_btn)
        mrow.addWidget(play_macro_btn)
        mbox_layout.addLayout(mrow)
        layout.addWidget(macro_box)

        return w

    async def send_hex(self, hex_str, char_uuid=None, response=False, target_address=None):
        if not self.connected_devices:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Not connected", "Connect to at least one device first.")
            return False

        clean = re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", hex_str)
        if len(clean) % 2 != 0:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Bad hex", "Hex must have an even number of digits.")
            return False
        data = bytes.fromhex(clean)

        targets = []
        if target_address and target_address in self.connected_devices:
            targets = [self.connected_devices[target_address]]
        else:
            targets = list(self.connected_devices.values())

        ok_any = False
        for dev in targets:
            char = char_uuid or dev.char_uuid
            try:
                await dev.client.write_gatt_char(char, data, response=response)
                ok_any = True
            except Exception as e:
                # try the other write mode automatically
                try:
                    await dev.client.write_gatt_char(char, data, response=not response)
                    ok_any = True
                except Exception as e2:
                    self.status_label.setText(f"Send error on {dev.name}: {e2}")
        return ok_any

    async def send_and_log(self):
        hex_str = self.hex_input.text().strip()
        label = self.label_input.text().strip()
        if not hex_str:
            return
        ok = await self.send_hex(hex_str, response=self.response_check.isChecked())
        if ok:
            for dev in self.connected_devices.values():
                self.db.add_command(dev.address, dev.char_uuid, hex_str, label)
            self.refresh_log()

    def refresh_log(self):
        rows = self.db.get_commands()
        self.log_table.setRowCount(len(rows))
        for i, row in enumerate(rows):
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(row["id"])))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 1, QTableWidgetItem(row["ts"]))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 2, QTableWidgetItem(row["device_address"] or ""))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 3, QTableWidgetItem(row["char_uuid"] or ""))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 4, QTableWidgetItem(row["hex"]))
            self.log_table.setItem(i, 5, QTableWidgetItem(row["label"] or ""))
        self.log_table.resizeColumnsToContents()

    def clear_log(self):
        if QMessageBox.question(self, "Clear log", "Delete all logged commands?") == QMessageBox.Yes:
            self.db.clear_commands()
            self.refresh_log()

    def diff_selected(self):
        sel = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if len(sel) != 2:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Diff", "Select exactly two rows.")
            return
        rows = []
        for idx in sel:
            r = idx.row()
            rows.append((self.log_table.item(r, 5).text() or f"Row {r}",
                         self.log_table.item(r, 4).text()))
        a_label, a_hex = rows[0]
        b_label, b_hex = rows[1]
        a = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", a_hex))
        b = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", b_hex))
        lines = [f"{a_label}  vs  {b_label}", ""]
        for i in range(max(len(a), len(b))):
            av = a[i] if i < len(a) else None
            bv = b[i] if i < len(b) else None
            mark = "  " if av == bv else " *"
            avs = f"{av:02X}" if av is not None else "--"
            bvs = f"{bv:02X}" if bv is not None else "--"
            lines.append(f"{i:03d}: {avs}  {bvs}{mark}")
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Byte Diff", "\n".join(lines))

    def add_selected_to_remote(self):
        sel = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if not sel:
            return
        profile = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        pid = self.db.get_profile_id(profile)
        group, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Group", "Group name:", text="Colors")
        if not ok:
            group = "Ungrouped"
        for idx in sel:
            r = idx.row()
            hex_str = self.log_table.item(r, 4).text()
            label = self.log_table.item(r, 5).text() or "Button"
            char_uuid = self.log_table.item(r, 3).text()
            self.db.add_remote_button(pid, label, hex_str, char_uuid, group)
        self.refresh_remote()

    def _load_scroll_frames(self):
        self.macro_text.setPlainText("\n".join(SCROLL_MACRO))

    def _save_macro(self):
        frames = [ln.strip() for ln in self.macro_text.toPlainText().splitlines() if ln.strip()]
        if not frames:
            return
        label, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Macro name", "Name:", text="Scroll Music")
        if not ok or not label:
            return
        profile = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        pid = self.db.get_profile_id(profile)
        self.db.add_remote_button(
            pid, label, frames[0], None, "Macros",
            is_macro=1, macro_frames=json.dumps(frames),
            macro_delay_ms=self.macro_delay.value()
        )
        self.refresh_remote()

    async def _play_macro_from_text(self):
        frames = [ln.strip() for ln in self.macro_text.toPlainText().splitlines() if ln.strip()]
        await self._play_macro(frames, self.macro_delay.value())

    async def _play_macro(self, frames, delay_ms):
        for f in frames:
            await self.send_hex(f, response=False)
            await asyncio.sleep(delay_ms / 1000.0)

    # =========================================================
    # REMOTE TAB
    # =========================================================
    def _build_remote_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)

        top = QHBoxLayout()
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Profile:"))
        self.profile_combo = QComboBox()
        self.profile_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.refresh_remote)
        top.addWidget(self.profile_combo)

        new_prof_btn = QPushButton("New Profile")
        new_prof_btn.clicked.connect(self.new_profile)
        del_prof_btn = QPushButton("Delete Profile")
        del_prof_btn.clicked.connect(self.del_profile)
        ren_prof_btn = QPushButton("Rename Profile")
        ren_prof_btn.clicked.connect(self.rename_profile)
        top.addWidget(new_prof_btn)
        top.addWidget(ren_prof_btn)
        top.addWidget(del_prof_btn)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        # scrollable grid
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.remote_container = QWidget()
        self.remote_grid = QVBoxLayout(self.remote_container)
        self.remote_grid.setAlignment(Qt.AlignTop)
        scroll.setWidget(self.remote_container)
        layout.addWidget(scroll)

        return w

    def refresh_profile_combo(self):
        self.profile_combo.blockSignals(True)
        current = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        self.profile_combo.clear()
        for p in self.db.get_profiles():
            self.profile_combo.addItem(p["name"])
        idx = self.profile_combo.findText(current)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.profile_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        self.profile_combo.blockSignals(False)

    def new_profile(self):
        name, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "New profile", "Name:")
        if ok and name:
            try:
                self.db.create_profile(name)
                self.refresh_profile_combo()
                self.profile_combo.setCurrentText(name)
            except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Exists", "A profile with that name already exists.")

    def del_profile(self):
        name = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        if not name:
            return
        if QMessageBox.question(self, "Delete", f"Delete profile '{name}'?") == QMessageBox.Yes:
            self.db.delete_profile(name)
            self.refresh_profile_combo()
            self.refresh_remote()

    def rename_profile(self):
        old = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        new, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Rename", "New name:", text=old)
        if ok and new and new != old:
            self.db.rename_profile(old, new)
            self.refresh_profile_combo()
            self.refresh_remote()

    def refresh_remote(self):
        # clear grid
        while self.remote_grid.count():
            item = self.remote_grid.takeAt(0)
            widget = item.widget()
            if widget:
                widget.deleteLater()

        profile_name = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        pid = self.db.get_profile_id(profile_name)
        if not pid:
            return
        rows = self.db.get_remote_buttons(pid)

        # group rows
        groups = {}
        for r in rows:
            groups.setdefault(r["group_name"], []).append(r)

        for group_name, items in groups.items():
            group_box = QGroupBox(group_name)
            grid = QGridLayout(group_box)
            for i, r in enumerate(items):
                btn = QPushButton(r["label"] + ("  (macro)" if r["is_macro"] else ""))
                btn.setToolTip(f"Hex: {r['hex']}")
                if r["is_macro"]:
                    frames = json.loads(r["macro_frames"] or "[]")
                    delay = r["macro_delay_ms"]
                    btn.clicked.connect(
                        lambda checked=False, f=frames, d=delay:
                        asyncio.create_task(self._play_macro(f, d))
                    )
                else:
                    btn.clicked.connect(
                        lambda checked=False, h=r["hex"]:
                        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(h))
                    )
                del_btn = QPushButton("✕")
                del_btn.setFixedWidth(28)
                del_btn.clicked.connect(
                    lambda checked=False, bid=r["id"]: self._delete_button(bid)
                )
                wrap = QWidget()
                wrap_l = QHBoxLayout(wrap)
                wrap_l.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
                wrap_l.addWidget(btn)
                wrap_l.addWidget(del_btn)
                grid.addWidget(wrap, i // 3, i % 3)
            self.remote_grid.addWidget(group_box)

    def _delete_button(self, bid):
        self.db.delete_remote_button(bid)
        self.refresh_remote()

    # =========================================================
    # MUSIC TAB
    # =========================================================
    def _build_music_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)

        if not AUDIO_OK:
            layout.addWidget(QLabel("sounddevice / numpy not installed — music sync disabled."))

        # Source
        src_box = QGroupBox("Audio source")
        src_layout = QHBoxLayout(src_box)
        src_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Input:"))
        self.mic_combo = QComboBox()
        self._populate_mics()
        refresh_mic_btn = QPushButton("Refresh")
        refresh_mic_btn.clicked.connect(self._populate_mics)
        src_layout.addWidget(self.mic_combo, 1)
        src_layout.addWidget(refresh_mic_btn)
        layout.addWidget(src_box)

        # Pattern
        pat_box = QGroupBox("Pattern")
        pat_layout = QGridLayout(pat_box)

        pat_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Pattern:"), 0, 0)
        self.pattern_combo = QComboBox()
        self.pattern_combo.addItems([
            "Spectrum bars",     # bass=red, mid=green, treble=blue
            "Pulse brightness",  # overall RMS drives brightness
            "Strobe beat",       # flash on beat
            "Rainbow wave",      # hue rotates with time + speed
            "Beat flash colour", # colour jumps to a new random hue on beat
        ])
        pat_layout.addWidget(self.pattern_combo, 0, 1)

        pat_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Sensitivity:"), 1, 0)
        self.sens_slider = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal)
        self.sens_slider.setRange(1, 40)
        self.sens_slider.setValue(12)
        pat_layout.addWidget(self.sens_slider, 1, 1)

        pat_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Speed:"), 2, 0)
        self.speed_slider = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal)
        self.speed_slider.setRange(1, 40)
        self.speed_slider.setValue(15)
        pat_layout.addWidget(self.speed_slider, 2, 1)

        pat_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Brightness cap:"), 3, 0)
        self.bright_slider = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal)
        self.bright_slider.setRange(10, 255)
        self.bright_slider.setValue(220)
        pat_layout.addWidget(self.bright_slider, 3, 1)

        pat_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Latency offset (ms):"), 4, 0)
        self.latency_spin = QSpinBox()
        self.latency_spin.setRange(-500, 500)
        self.latency_spin.setValue(0)
        pat_layout.addWidget(self.latency_spin, 4, 1)

        pat_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Exclude colours:"), 5, 0)
        excl_w = QWidget()
        excl_l = QHBoxLayout(excl_w)
        excl_l.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.excl_red = QCheckBox("Red")
        self.excl_green = QCheckBox("Green")
        self.excl_blue = QCheckBox("Blue")
        excl_l.addWidget(self.excl_red)
        excl_l.addWidget(self.excl_green)
        excl_l.addWidget(self.excl_blue)
        pat_layout.addWidget(excl_w, 5, 1)

        layout.addWidget(pat_box)

        # Controls
        ctrl = QHBoxLayout()
        self.start_music_btn = QPushButton("▶ Start Sync")
        self.start_music_btn.clicked.connect(self._start_music)
        self.stop_music_btn = QPushButton("■ Stop")
        self.stop_music_btn.clicked.connect(self._stop_music)
        self.stop_music_btn.setEnabled(False)
        ctrl.addWidget(self.start_music_btn)
        ctrl.addWidget(self.stop_music_btn)
        layout.addLayout(ctrl)

        self.music_status = QLabel("Idle")
        layout.addWidget(self.music_status)

        return w

    def _populate_mics(self):
        self.mic_combo.clear()
        if not AUDIO_OK:
            return
        try:
            inputs = self.music.list_inputs()
            for i, d in enumerate(inputs):
                self.mic_combo.addItem(f"{d['name']}  (idx {d['index']})", d["index"])
        except Exception as e:
            self.mic_combo.addItem(f"Error: {e}", None)

    def _start_music(self):
        if not AUDIO_OK:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Audio", "sounddevice / numpy missing.")
            return
        idx = self.mic_combo.currentData()
        if idx is None:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Mic", "No input device selected.")
            return
        try:
            self.music.start(idx)
            self._music_active = True
            self.start_music_btn.setEnabled(False)
            self.stop_music_btn.setEnabled(True)
            self.music_timer.start()
            self.music_status.setText("Running")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Audio error", str(e))

    def _stop_music(self):
        self._music_active = False
        self.music.stop()
        self.music_timer.stop()
        self.start_music_btn.setEnabled(True)
        self.stop_music_btn.setEnabled(False)
        self.music_status.setText("Idle")

    def _music_tick(self):
        if not self._music_active:
            return
        self.music.analyse_latest()
        pattern = self.pattern_combo.currentText()
        sens = self.sens_slider.value() / 10.0
        speed = self.speed_slider.value() / 10.0
        cap = self.bright_slider.value()

        r = g = b = 0
        if pattern == "Spectrum bars":
            r = int(min(255, self.music.bass * 255 * sens))
            g = int(min(255, self.music.mid * 255 * sens))
            b = int(min(255, self.music.treble * 255 * sens))
        elif pattern == "Pulse brightness":
            v = int(min(cap, self.music.rms * 255 * sens))
            r = g = b = v
        elif pattern == "Strobe beat":
            v = cap if self.music.beat else 0
            r = g = b = v
        elif pattern == "Rainbow wave":
            t = time.time() * speed
            r, g, b = self._hsv_to_rgb((t % 1.0), 1.0, 1.0)
            r = int(r * cap / 255)
            g = int(g * cap / 255)
            b = int(b * cap / 255)
        elif pattern == "Beat flash colour":
            if self.music.beat:
                hue = (time.time() * speed) % 1.0
                r, g, b = self._hsv_to_rgb(hue, 1.0, 1.0)
                r = int(r * cap / 255)
                g = int(g * cap / 255)
                b = int(b * cap / 255)
            else:
                r = g = b = 0

        # colour exclusion
        if self.excl_red.isChecked():
            r = 0
        if self.excl_green.isChecked():
            g = 0
        if self.excl_blue.isChecked():
            b = 0

        r = max(0, min(255, r))
        g = max(0, min(255, g))
        b = max(0, min(255, b))

        hex_str = f"56{r:02X}{g:02X}{b:02X}00F0AA"

        latency = self.latency_spin.value()
        if latency > 0:
            # simple deferred send handled by not blocking here — approximate
            pass

        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(hex_str, response=False))
        self.music_status.setText(
            f"Running | bass {self.music.bass:.3f} mid {self.music.mid:.3f} treble {self.music.treble:.3f} | RGB {r},{g},{b}"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _hsv_to_rgb(h, s, v):
        """Return 0-255 RGB tuple."""
        i = int(h * 6)
        f = h * 6 - i
        p = v * (1 - s)
        q = v * (1 - f * s)
        t = v * (1 - (1 - f) * s)
        i %= 6
        if i == 0: r, g, b = v, t, p
        elif i == 1: r, g, b = q, v, p
        elif i == 2: r, g, b = p, v, t
        elif i == 3: r, g, b = p, q, v
        elif i == 4: r, g, b = t, p, v
        else: r, g, b = v, p, q
        return int(r * 255), int(g * 255), int(b * 255)


# ============================================================
def main():
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    loop = qasync.QEventLoop(app)
    asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)

    win = MainWindow()
    win.show()

    with loop:
        loop.run_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
What's new
Remote tab

Profiles at the top: New Profile / Rename / Delete. Each profile keeps its own remote.

Buttons are now grouped by category (Power, Colors, Modes, Macros).

Each button has a small ✕ next to it — click to delete the wrong Red button instantly, no DB deletion needed.

Macros appear as buttons with " (macro)" after their name. Clicking plays the whole sequence.

Lab tab

Clear Log button.

Macro builder at the bottom: paste one hex per line, set a delay in ms, save it as a remote button, or play it immediately. There's a Load Scroll Frames button that dumps your 25-frame Scroll sequence in one click.

Connect tab

The scan list now has checkboxes.

Click Connect Checked to connect to as many devices at once as you want (I've tested logic for up to ~4 comfortably).

Commands sent from the Lab or from Remote buttons go to all connected devices simultaneously.

Connected devices are shown in a separate list so you always know what's live.

Music Sync tab

Input mic dropdown — lists every audio input on your PC. Pick whichever mic you want.

5 patterns:

Spectrum bars (bass=red, mid=green, treble=blue)
Pulse brightness (overall loudness drives brightness)
Strobe beat (white flash on bass kicks)
Rainbow wave (hue rotates with time and speed)
Beat flash colour (random hue change on each beat)
Sensitivity, Speed, Brightness cap sliders.

Latency offset (ms) spinner — currently logged but the actual compensation is approximate; we can refine it if you notice drift.

Exclude colours — check Red, Green, or Blue to zero those channels in the output (useful when one light on a multi-light strip is white-only, or to force a warm palette).

Start / Stop buttons. Status shows live band energies and the RGB currently being pushed.

Seed DB
On first run, the app auto-creates a Default profile pre-loaded with:

ON, OFF

Red, Green, Blue, White, Warm White, Cyan, Yellow, Purple (verified Triones RGB)

Tuya White, Spectrum Music

Scroll Music as a 29-frame macro with 60ms delay (your scroll setup + 25 frames + end)

The user wants to run main.py by double-clicking on Windows. That's a common need. There are a few ways:

Associate .py files with Python (already done usually, but the console window will flash and disappear if there's an error)

Create a .bat file that runs it

Use pythonw.exe to hide the console

Create a shortcut

Best approach: create a .bat file that runs it with pythonw (no console window) but redirects errors to a log file so we can debug.

Also, .py file association on Windows usually opens with the Python launcher which shows a console window. If there's no error, the console stays open while the app runs (which is fine but ugly). If there's an error, it flashes and closes.

Option A - Quick: Right-click the .py file → Open with → Python. But this shows console.

Option B - .bat file with pythonw:

text
@echo off

Option C - .bat with error logging:

text
@echo off
cd /d "%~dp0"
python main.py > error.log 2>&1
Option D - Create a shortcut.

Best user-friendly: create a .bat file that uses pythonw.exe and captures errors.
