Yes — a few ways, from quick-and-dirty to proper app. I'll give you the best one, then the fallback, then the "real app" option.

Option 1: A .bat launcher (best for now)
This launches the app without a console window and quietly logs any error to a file so you can debug if something breaks.

In the same folder as main.py, right-click → New → Text Document.

Name it Launch LumenForge.bat (make sure Windows isn't hiding the .txt extension — if it is, rename it after saving).

Open it in Notepad and paste this:

bat
@echo off
cd /d "%~dp0"
start "" pythonw main.py 2> "lumenforge_error.log"
Save and close.

Double-click Launch LumenForge.bat. The app should open with no black window.

Why pythonw instead of python: python opens a console window every time. pythonw is the "windowless" version that comes with Python — the Qt app shows, no console.

Why the error log: if the app fails to start, you won't see why (no console). The 2> lumenforge_error.log captures the error text to a file in the same folder. If the app ever doesn't open, open that log and paste it to me.

To make it double-clickable without showing .bat extension (optional polish): in File Explorer, View → Show → File name extensions, then rename to LumenForge.bat. Windows may prompt to confirm — that's fine, it's a batch file.

Option 2: Right-click the .py directly
.py files are usually already associated with Python. Double-clicking main.py should work — but it opens a console window that stays open the whole time. If the app crashes on launch, the console flashes and disappears before you can read the error. That's why Option 1 is better.

If double-clicking does nothing, Python isn't associated. Install Python from python.org (the official installer does this) and check "Add Python to PATH" during install.

Option 3: A real standalone .exe (later, once we're done building)
This turns the whole thing into a single file you can share, move to another PC, or pin to Start without needing Python installed. It's the "proper app" answer.

Two commands once you're ready:

bat
pip install pyinstaller
pyinstaller --noconsole --name LumenForge --onefile main.py
Result: a dist\LumenForge.exe you can double-click anywhere. Downsides:

The first build takes 2–5 minutes.

PySide6 apps built with --onefile are big (~150 MB) and slow to launch (5–10 seconds unpacking).

Bluetooth can be flaky inside a frozen exe unless you also bundle the right DLLs, which sometimes takes tweaking.

My recommendation: use Option 1 (the .bat) while we're still iterating. Once the app is feature-complete and stable, I'll walk you through PyInstaller properly and get you the .exe.

Optional polish: a nice icon and shortcut
Right-click Launch LumenForge.bat → Send to → Desktop (create shortcut).

Rename the shortcut to LumenForge.

🎉 WE CRACKED IT
The command is:

text
BC 04 06  HH HH  BB BB  00 00  55
         \____/  \____/
          hue     brightness
H H = hue, big-endian, 0–360

0000 = red, 003C = yellow, 0078 = green, 00F0 = blue

B B = brightness, big-endian, ~1000 is the sweet spot

03E8 (1000) = full color

01F4 (500) = dim, white LEDs dominate → looks warm white

07D0 (2000) = overdriven → strip defaults to white

Rule: hue 0–360 in 0x0000–0x0168, brightness around 0x03E8 (1000). Anything above ~1200 breaks.

text
BC0406007801F4000055     ← green at 500 (dim green?)
BC0406007801F4000055     ← green at 500 again
BC0406007800C8000055     ← green at 200 (very dim?)
BC0406007807D0000055     ← green at 2000 (breaks to white?)

# main.py — AymashTain LED RGB Remote  v1.0 (MR Star)
import sys, os, asyncio, sqlite3, datetime, re, json, queue, time, traceback, threading, math, colorsys

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QApplication, QMainWindow, QTabWidget, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout,
    QPushButton, QListWidget, QListWidgetItem, QLabel, QComboBox, QLineEdit,
    QCheckBox, QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QMessageBox, QGridLayout,
    QSlider, QGroupBox, QInputDialog, QScrollArea, QTextEdit, QSpinBox,
    QFileDialog, QMenu, QSizePolicy
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, QRectF, QPointF, Signal
from PySide6.QtGui import (
    QAction, QIcon, QPainter, QColor, QPixmap, QConicalGradient,
    QRadialGradient, QPen, QBrush
)
from bleak import BleakScanner, BleakClient
import qasync

sd = None; np = None; AUDIO_TRIED = False; AUDIO_OK = True
def load_audio():
    global sd, np, AUDIO_TRIED, AUDIO_OK
    if AUDIO_TRIED: return AUDIO_OK
    AUDIO_TRIED = True
    try:
        import sounddevice as _sd; import numpy as _np
        sd = _sd; np = _np; AUDIO_OK = True
    except Exception as e:
        log_error(f"audio import failed: {e}"); AUDIO_OK = False
    return AUDIO_OK

APP_NAME = "AymashTain LED RGB Remote"
APP_VERSION = "v1.0-MRStar"
LOG_FILE = "lumenforge_errors.log"
ICON_NAMES = ["AymashTain.ico", "aymashtaine.ico", "lumenforge.ico", "icon.ico"]

def app_dir():
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False): return os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
    return os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def log_error(msg):
    try:
        path = os.path.join(app_dir(), LOG_FILE)
        if os.path.exists(path) and os.path.getsize(path) > 2 * 1024 * 1024:
            try: os.replace(path, path + ".old")
            except Exception: pass
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(f"[{ts}] {msg}\n")
    except Exception: pass

def excepthook(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb):
    text = "".join(traceback.format_exception(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb))
    log_error("UNCAUGHT:\n" + text)
    try: QMessageBox.critical(None, "AymashTain error", "Error logged; app continues.")
    except Exception: pass

def find_icon():
    d = app_dir()
    try: files = os.listdir(d)
    except Exception as e:
        log_error(f"Icon: cannot list {d}: {e}"); return None
    for name in ICON_NAMES:
        if name in files:
            log_error(f"Icon: exact {name}"); return os.path.join(d, name)
    lower = {f.lower(): f for f in files}
    for name in ICON_NAMES:
        if name.lower() in lower:
            log_error(f"Icon: case-insensitive {lower[name.lower()]}")
            return os.path.join(d, lower[name.lower()])
    icos = [f for f in files if f.lower().endswith(".ico")]
    if icos:
        log_error(f"Icon: fallback {icos[0]}"); return os.path.join(d, icos[0])
    log_error("Icon: NA"); return None


# ============================================================
# MR STAR PROTOCOL
# ============================================================
CMD_ON   = "bc01010155"
CMD_OFF  = "bc01010055"
CMD_STATIC = "bc04010055"   # exits animation modes, enters static/color mode

def mrstar_color(r, g, b, brightness=1000):
    """RGB 0-255 → MR Star BC0406 color command.
       Format: BC 04 06 HH HH BB BB 00 00 55
       Hue in 0-360, brightness typically 1000."""
    r_n, g_n, b_n = r / 255.0, g / 255.0, b / 255.0
    h, s, v = colorsys.rgb_to_hsv(r_n, g_n, b_n)
    hue = int(round(h * 360)) % 361
    # scale brightness by v so darker colors get dimmer values
    bri = max(1, min(1000, int(brightness * v)))
    return f"BC0406{hue:04X}{bri:04X}000055"

# Scroll — the animation macro that caused all the trouble
SCROLL_SETUP = ["BC0F010155", "BC11010455"]
SCROLL_DATA = [
    "BC0406000003E8000055", "BC0406013E0032000055", "BC04060131002B000055",
    "BC040600E200A8000055", "BC040600E400B8000055", "BC040600E60091000055",
    "BC040600E90046000055", "BC040600F00027000055", "BC040600F00023000055",
    "BC040600F00023000055", "BC040600FF003E000055", "BC040601050081000055",
    "BC0406011B00EB000055", "BC0406013D01A3000055", "BC0406014D0230000055",
    "BC04060161026F000055", "BC0406001B02FC000055", "BC0406002C0372000055",
    "BC0406003E02E1000055", "BC0406005A0215000055", "BC0406008701BF000055",
    "BC040600B00215000055", "BC040600C90296000055", "BC040600DF0314000055",
    "BC040600F7033B000055",
]
SCROLL_END = ["BC0F010155", "BC11010455", "bc04010055"]  # ends with static reset
SCROLL_MACRO = SCROLL_SETUP + SCROLL_DATA + SCROLL_END


# ============================================================
# DATABASE
# ============================================================
class DB:
    def __init__(self, path=None):
        if path is None: path = os.path.join(app_dir(), "lumenforge.db")
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(path); self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self._init()

    def _init(self):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profiles (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            protocol TEXT DEFAULT 'MRStar', created_at TEXT NOT NULL)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commands (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, ts TEXT NOT NULL,
            device_address TEXT, char_uuid TEXT, hex TEXT NOT NULL,
            label TEXT, notes TEXT)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS remote_buttons (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, profile_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            label TEXT NOT NULL, hex TEXT NOT NULL, char_uuid TEXT,
            group_name TEXT DEFAULT 'Ungrouped', sort_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            is_macro INTEGER DEFAULT 0, macro_frames TEXT,
            macro_delay_ms INTEGER DEFAULT 60,
            FOREIGN KEY(profile_id) REFERENCES profiles(id))""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS devices (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, address TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            name TEXT, last_char_uuid TEXT, last_seen TEXT)""")
        self.conn.commit()
        if not self.get_profiles():
            self._seed()

    def _seed(self):
        pid = self.create_profile("Default", "MRStar")
        for i, (g, l, h) in enumerate([
            ("Power",  "Turn ON",  CMD_ON),
            ("Power",  "Turn OFF", CMD_OFF),
            ("Power",  "Static Mode", CMD_STATIC),
            ("Colors", "Red",    mrstar_color(255, 0, 0)),
            ("Colors", "Green",  mrstar_color(0, 255, 0)),
            ("Colors", "Blue",   mrstar_color(0, 0, 255)),
            ("Colors", "Yellow", mrstar_color(255, 255, 0)),
            ("Colors", "Cyan",   mrstar_color(0, 255, 255)),
            ("Colors", "Magenta",mrstar_color(255, 0, 255)),
            ("Colors", "White",  mrstar_color(255, 255, 255)),
        ]):
            self.add_remote_button(pid, l, h, None, g, i)
        self.add_remote_button(
            pid, "Scroll (locks until Static)", SCROLL_MACRO[0], None, "Effects", 100,
            is_macro=1, macro_frames=json.dumps(SCROLL_MACRO), macro_delay_ms=55)

    def get_profiles(self):
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM profiles ORDER BY name").fetchall()
    def get_profile(self, name):
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM profiles WHERE name=?", (name,)).fetchone()
    def get_profile_id(self, name):
        r = self.get_profile(name); return r["id"] if r else None
    def create_profile(self, name, protocol="MRStar"):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        self.conn.execute("INSERT INTO profiles (name, protocol, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?)",
                          (name, protocol, ts))
        self.conn.commit(); return self.get_profile_id(name)
    def delete_profile(self, name):
        pid = self.get_profile_id(name)
        if pid:
            self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM remote_buttons WHERE profile_id=?", (pid,))
            self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM profiles WHERE id=?", (pid,))
            self.conn.commit()
    def rename_profile(self, old, new):
        self.conn.execute("UPDATE profiles SET name=? WHERE name=?", (new, old)); self.conn.commit()
    def add_command(self, dev, ch, h, label="", notes=""):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("INSERT INTO commands (ts,device_address,char_uuid,hex,label,notes) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                  (ts, dev, ch, h, label, notes))
        self.conn.commit(); return c.lastrowid
    def get_commands(self, limit=500):
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM commands ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", (limit,)).fetchall()
    def clear_commands(self):
        self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM commands"); self.conn.commit()
    def add_remote_button(self, pid, label, hex_s, ch, group="Ungrouped", order=0,
                          is_macro=0, macro_frames=None, macro_delay_ms=60):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("""INSERT INTO remote_buttons
            (profile_id,label,hex,char_uuid,group_name,sort_order,is_macro,macro_frames,macro_delay_ms)
            VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
            (pid, label, hex_s, ch, group, order, is_macro, macro_frames, macro_delay_ms))
        self.conn.commit(); return c.lastrowid
    def get_remote_buttons(self, pid):
        return self.conn.execute("""SELECT * FROM remote_buttons WHERE profile_id=?
            ORDER BY group_name, sort_order, id""", (pid,)).fetchall()
    def get_remote_button(self, bid):
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM remote_buttons WHERE id=?", (bid,)).fetchone()
    def delete_remote_button(self, bid):
        self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM remote_buttons WHERE id=?", (bid,)); self.conn.commit()
    def update_remote_button(self, bid, label, hex_s, group):
        self.conn.execute("UPDATE remote_buttons SET label=?, hex=?, group_name=? WHERE id=?",
                          (label, hex_s, group, bid)); self.conn.commit()
    def clone_remote_button(self, bid):
        r = self.get_remote_button(bid)
        if r: return self.add_remote_button(r["profile_id"], r["label"] + " (copy)",
            r["hex"], r["char_uuid"], r["group_name"], r["sort_order"]+1,
            r["is_macro"], r["macro_frames"], r["macro_delay_ms"])
    def upsert_device(self, address, name, ch):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        self.conn.execute("""INSERT INTO devices (address,name,last_char_uuid,last_seen)
            VALUES (?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(address) DO UPDATE SET
            name=excluded.name,last_char_uuid=excluded.last_char_uuid,last_seen=excluded.last_seen""",
            (address, name, ch, ts)); self.conn.commit()
    def close(self):
        try: self.conn.close()
        except Exception: pass


class ConnectedDevice:
    def __init__(self, address, name, client, char_uuid):
        self.address = address; self.name = name; self.client = client
        self.char_uuid = char_uuid; self.status = "connected"; self.last_ok = None


class ColorWheel(QWidget):
    colorPicked = Signal(int, int, int)
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setMinimumSize(240, 240)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Expanding)
        self._value = 1.0; self._hue = 0.0; self._sat = 1.0
        self._pixmap = None
        self._last_emit = 0.0
        self._build_wheel()
    def resizeEvent(self, e):
        self._pixmap = None; self._build_wheel(); super().resizeEvent(e)
    def _build_wheel(self):
        size = max(200, min(self.width(), self.height()))
        pix = QPixmap(size, size); pix.fill(Qt.transparent)
        p = QPainter(pix); p.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
        rect = QRectF(2, 2, size-4, size-4)
        conic = QConicalGradient(size/2, size/2, 0)
        for i in range(361):
            conic.setColorAt(i/360.0, QColor.fromHsvF((i/360.0)%1.0, 1.0, 1.0))
        p.setBrush(QBrush(conic)); p.setPen(Qt.NoPen); p.drawEllipse(rect)
        radial = QRadialGradient(size/2, size/2, size/2)
        radial.setColorAt(0.0, QColor(255,255,255,255))
        radial.setColorAt(1.0, QColor(255,255,255,0))
        p.setBrush(QBrush(radial)); p.drawEllipse(rect); p.end()
        self._pixmap = pix
    def set_value(self, v): self._value = v; self._emit_current()
    def _emit_current(self):
        r, g, b = self._hsv(self._hue, self._sat, self._value)
        self.colorPicked.emit(r, g, b)
    @staticmethod
    def _hsv(h, s, v):
        r, g, b = colorsys.hsv_to_rgb(h, s, v)
        return int(r*255), int(g*255), int(b*255)
    def paintEvent(self, e):
        if self._pixmap is None: self._build_wheel()
        p = QPainter(self); p.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
        w, h = self.width(), self.height()
        size = self._pixmap.width()
        x, y = (w-size)/2, (h-size)/2
        p.drawPixmap(int(x), int(y), self._pixmap)
        cx, cy = x+size/2, y+size/2
        r_max = size/2-2
        ang = self._hue*2*math.pi; rr = self._sat*r_max
        px = cx+math.cos(ang)*rr; py = cy+math.sin(ang)*rr
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor(255,255,255), 2)); p.setBrush(Qt.NoBrush)
        p.drawEllipse(QPointF(px, py), 8, 8)
    def mousePressEvent(self, e): self._pick(e.position())
    def mouseMoveEvent(self, e):
        if e.buttons() & Qt.LeftButton: self._pick(e.position())
    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, e):
        # force a final send on release
        self._last_emit = 0
        self._emit_current()
    def _pick(self, pos):
        w, h = self.width(), self.height()
        size = self._pixmap.width() if self._pixmap else min(w, h)
        cx, cy = w/2, h/2
        dx, dy = pos.x()-cx, pos.y()-cy
        r = math.hypot(dx, dy)
        r_max = size/2-2
        if r > r_max: r = r_max
        self._hue = (math.atan2(dy, dx)/(2*math.pi)) % 1.0
        self._sat = r/r_max
        self.update()
        # throttle: max ~15Hz
        now = time.time()
        if now - self._last_emit < 0.06: return
        self._last_emit = now
        self._emit_current()


class StripView(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, rows=15, cols=10, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.rows = rows; self.cols = cols
        self.base = QColor(0, 0, 0); self.override = None
        self.setMinimumHeight(rows*14+14)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)
    def set_color(self, r, g, b):
        self.base = QColor(r, g, b); self.override = None; self.update()
    def paintEvent(self, e):
        p = QPainter(self)
        w, h = self.width(), self.height()
        cw, ch = w/self.cols, (h-10)/self.rows
        for r in range(self.rows):
            for c in range(self.cols):
                x, y = c*cw, 10+r*ch
                p.fillRect(QRectF(x+1, y+1, cw-2, ch-2), self.base)
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor(120,120,130), 1))
        p.drawLine(0, 5, w, 5); p.drawLine(0, h-4, w, h-4)


class MusicEngine:
    def __init__(self, sr=22050, block=1024):
        self.sr = sr; self.block = block
        self.audio_q = queue.Queue(maxsize=8)
        self.stream = None; self.file_stop = threading.Event()
        self._file_data = None; self._file_pos = 0
        self.bass = self.mid = self.treble = self.rms = 0.0
        self.beat = False; self._last_bass = 0.0; self._cooldown = 0.0
    def list_inputs(self):
        if not load_audio(): return []
        out = []
        try:
            hostapis = sd.query_hostapis()
            for i, d in enumerate(sd.query_devices()):
                if d["max_input_channels"] > 0:
                    api = hostapis[d["hostapi"]]["name"] if d.get("hostapi") is not None else "?"
                    out.append((i, d["name"], api))
        except Exception as e: log_error(f"list_inputs: {e}")
        return out
    def start_mic(self, idx):
        if not load_audio(): raise RuntimeError("audio unavailable")
        def cb(indata, frames, t, status):
            try: self.audio_q.put_nowait(indata.copy())
            except queue.Full: pass
        self.stream = sd.InputStream(device=idx, channels=1, samplerate=self.sr,
                                     blocksize=self.block, callback=cb)
        self.stream.start()
    def start_system_loopback(self):
        if not load_audio(): raise RuntimeError("audio unavailable")
        if sys.platform != "win32": raise RuntimeError("Windows only")
        out_dev = sd.default.device[1]
        info = sd.query_devices(out_dev)
        settings = sd.WasapiSettings(loopback=True)
        def cb(indata, frames, t, status):
            try: self.audio_q.put_nowait(indata.copy())
            except queue.Full: pass
        self.stream = sd.InputStream(device=out_dev,
                                     channels=min(2, info["max_output_channels"]),
                                     samplerate=self.sr, blocksize=self.block,
                                     extra_settings=settings, callback=cb)
        self.stream.start()
    def start_file(self, path):
        if not load_audio(): raise RuntimeError("audio unavailable")
        import soundfile as sf
        self.file_stop.clear()
        data, sr = sf.read(path, dtype="float32", always_2d=True)
        if data.shape[1] > 1: data = data.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        self._file_data = data; self._file_pos = 0
        def cb(outdata, frames, t, status):
            if self.file_stop.is_set():
                outdata[:] = 0; raise sd.CallbackStop
            rem = len(self._file_data) - self._file_pos
            if rem <= 0: outdata[:] = 0; raise sd.CallbackStop
            take = min(frames, rem)
            outdata[:take] = self._file_data[self._file_pos:self._file_pos+take]
            outdata[take:] = 0
            self._file_pos += take
            try: self.audio_q.put_nowait(outdata[:take].copy())
            except queue.Full: pass
        self.stream = sd.OutputStream(samplerate=sr, channels=1, callback=cb)
        self.stream.start()
    def stop(self):
        self.file_stop.set()
        if self.stream:
            try: self.stream.stop(); self.stream.close()
            except Exception: pass
            self.stream = None
    def analyse(self):
        if not load_audio(): return
        latest = None
        while True:
            try: latest = self.audio_q.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty: break
        if latest is None: return
        s = latest[:, 0].astype(np.float32)
        if len(s) < 64: return
        w = s * np.hanning(len(s))
        fft = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(w))
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(s), 1.0/self.sr)
        def band(lo, hi):
            m = (freqs >= lo) & (freqs < hi)
            return float(np.mean(fft[m])) if np.any(m) else 0.0
        self.bass = band(20, 250); self.mid = band(250, 2000); self.treble = band(2000, 8000)
        self.rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(s**2)))
        now = time.time()
        self.beat = (self.bass > self._last_bass*1.6 and self.bass > 0.02 and now > self._cooldown)
        if self.beat: self._cooldown = now + 0.15
        self._last_bass = 0.85*self._last_bass + 0.15*self.bass


class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle(f"{APP_NAME}  {APP_VERSION}")
        self.resize(1360, 900)
        self.db = DB()
        self.connected_devices = {}
        self.scan_results = []
        self.music = MusicEngine()
        self._music_active = False; self._closing = False
        self._last_command = None
        self._last_color_send = 0.0
        self.send_delay_ms = 120  # per-device stagger
        self.brightness_ceiling = 1000

        self.tabs = QTabWidget(); self.setCentralWidget(self.tabs)
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_connect_tab(), "Connect")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_remote_tab(), "Remote")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_music_tab(), "Music Sync")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_lab_tab(), "Lab")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_layout_tab(), "Visual Layout")

        mb = self.menuBar(); view_menu = mb.addMenu("View")
        self.dark_action = QAction("Dark mode", self, checkable=True)
        self.dark_action.setChecked(self._windows_dark())
        self.dark_action.toggled.connect(self._apply_theme)
        view_menu.addAction(self.dark_action)
        self._apply_theme(self.dark_action.isChecked())

        self.refresh_log(); self.refresh_profile_combo(); self.refresh_remote()

        self.music_timer = QTimer(); self.music_timer.setInterval(80)
        self.music_timer.timeout.connect(self._music_tick)
        self.heartbeat_timer = QTimer(); self.heartbeat_timer.setInterval(4000)
        self.heartbeat_timer.timeout.connect(self._heartbeat)
        self.heartbeat_timer.start()

        self.statusBar().showMessage("Ready — MR Star mode")

    def _windows_dark(self):
        try:
            import winreg
            k = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
                r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Themes\Personalize")
            v, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(k, "AppsUseLightTheme"); return v == 0
        except Exception: return False

    def _apply_theme(self, dark):
        if dark:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QWidget { background:#1c1c1e; color:#f2f2f7; }
                QTabWidget::pane { border:1px solid #2c2c2e; background:#1c1c1e; }
                QTabBar::tab { background:#2c2c2e; color:#f2f2f7; padding:6px 14px; }
                QTabBar::tab:selected { background:#3a3a3c; }
                QPushButton { background:#2c2c2e; border:1px solid #3a3a3c;
                    padding:6px 10px; border-radius:6px; }
                QPushButton:hover { background:#3a3a3c; }
                QLineEdit, QComboBox, QSpinBox, QTextEdit, QListWidget, QTableWidget {
                    background:#2c2c2e; color:#f2f2f7; border:1px solid #3a3a3c; border-radius:4px; }
                QGroupBox { border:1px solid #3a3a3c; border-radius:6px; margin-top:10px; }
                QGroupBox::title { subcontrol-origin: margin; left:10px; padding:0 4px; }
                QHeaderView::section { background:#2c2c2e; color:#f2f2f7; padding:4px; }
            """)
        else: self.setStyleSheet("")

    def _heartbeat(self):
        changed = False
        for dev in list(self.connected_devices.values()):
            try: still = getattr(dev.client, "is_connected", True)
            except Exception: still = False
            new = "connected" if still else "disconnected"
            if new != dev.status:
                dev.status = new; changed = True
                if new == "disconnected": log_error(f"HEARTBEAT: {dev.name} dropped")
        if changed:
            self._refresh_connected_list(); self._refresh_layout()
            if self._music_active and not any(d.status == "connected"
                                              for d in self.connected_devices.values()):
                self._stop_music()

    # ---------------- CONNECT ----------------
    def _build_connect_tab(self):
        w = QWidget(); layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        top = QHBoxLayout()
        for txt, slot in [("Scan", self._scan), ("Connect Checked", self._connect_checked),
                          ("Disconnect All", self._disconnect_all),
                          ("Forget Disconnected", self._forget_disconnected)]:
            b = QPushButton(txt); b.clicked.connect(slot); top.addWidget(b)
        layout.addLayout(top)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Double-click a disconnected device in the list to reconnect it."))
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Scan results (check, then Connect Checked):"))
        self.device_list = QListWidget(); layout.addWidget(self.device_list)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Connected devices:"))
        self.connected_list = QListWidget()
        self.connected_list.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self._reconnect_clicked)
        layout.addWidget(self.connected_list)
        return w

    def _scan(self): asyncio.create_task(self.scan_ble())

    async def scan_ble(self):
        try:
            self.scan_results = await BleakScanner.discover(timeout=6.0)
            self.device_list.clear()
            for d in self.scan_results:
                it = QListWidgetItem(f"{d.name or 'Unknown'}  |  {d.address}")
                it.setFlags(it.flags() | Qt.ItemIsUserCheckable)
                it.setCheckState(Qt.Unchecked)
                it.setData(Qt.UserRole, d.address)
                it.setData(Qt.UserRole + 1, d.name or "Unknown")
                self.device_list.addItem(it)
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"Found {len(self.scan_results)}")
        except Exception as e:
            log_error(f"scan: {e}")

    def _connect_checked(self): asyncio.create_task(self.connect_checked())

    async def _connect_one(self, address, name):
        try:
            client = BleakClient(address, timeout=15.0)
            await client.connect()
            candidates = []
            for svc in client.services:
                for ch in svc.characteristics:
                    if "write" in ch.properties or "write-without-response" in ch.properties:
                        candidates.append(ch.uuid)
            char_uuid = None
            for suf in ["fff3", "ffe1", "fff2"]:
                for c in candidates:
                    if suf in c.lower(): char_uuid = c; break
                if char_uuid: break
            if not char_uuid and candidates: char_uuid = candidates[0]
            if not char_uuid:
                await client.disconnect(); return False
            self.connected_devices[address] = ConnectedDevice(address, name, client, char_uuid)
            self.db.upsert_device(address, name, char_uuid)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            log_error(f"connect {name}: {e}"); return False

    async def connect_checked(self):
        checked = []
        for i in range(self.device_list.count()):
            it = self.device_list.item(i)
            if it.checkState() == Qt.Checked:
                checked.append((it.data(Qt.UserRole), it.data(Qt.UserRole + 1)))
        if not checked:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Connect", "Check at least one device."); return
        for address, name in checked:
            if address in self.connected_devices: continue
            await self._connect_one(address, name)
        self._refresh_connected_list(); self._refresh_layout()
        self.statusBar().showMessage(f"Connected to {len(self.connected_devices)}")

    async def _reconnect_addr(self, addr):
        # find name in scan results if we have it
        name = None
        for d in self.scan_results:
            if d.address == addr: name = d.name or "Unknown"; break
        if name is None: name = addr
        await self._connect_one(addr, name)
        self._refresh_connected_list(); self._refresh_layout()

    def _reconnect_clicked(self, item):
        text = item.text()
        # format: "●  name | addr | status"
        try:
            parts = text.split("|")
            addr = parts[1].strip()
            if addr in self.connected_devices and self.connected_devices[addr].status == "connected":
                return
            asyncio.create_task(self._reconnect_addr(addr))
        except Exception as e:
            log_error(f"reconnect parse: {e}")

    def _disconnect_all(self): asyncio.create_task(self.disconnect_all())

    async def disconnect_all(self):
        for dev in list(self.connected_devices.values()):
            try: await dev.client.disconnect()
            except Exception: pass
        self.connected_devices.clear()
        self._refresh_connected_list(); self._refresh_layout()
        if self._music_active: self._stop_music()
        self.statusBar().showMessage("Disconnected")

    def _forget_disconnected(self):
        for a in [a for a, d in self.connected_devices.items() if d.status != "connected"]:
            del self.connected_devices[a]
        self._refresh_connected_list(); self._refresh_layout()

    def _refresh_connected_list(self):
        self.connected_list.clear()
        for dev in self.connected_devices.values():
            tag = {"connected": "●", "disconnected": "○"}.get(dev.status, "?")
            self.connected_list.addItem(f"{tag}  {dev.name}  |  {dev.address}  |  {dev.status}")

    # ---------------- SEND ----------------
    async def send_hex(self, hex_s, char_uuid=None, response=False, address=None):
        if not self.connected_devices:
            self.statusBar().showMessage("Not connected"); return False
        clean = re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", hex_s)
        if len(clean) % 2:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Bad hex", "Odd length."); return False
        data = bytes.fromhex(clean)
        targets = [self.connected_devices[address]] if address in self.connected_devices \
                  else [d for d in self.connected_devices.values() if d.status == "connected"]
        if not targets: return False

        results = []
        # SEQUENTIAL with per-device delay so 3 strips don't collide on the radio
        for i, dev in enumerate(targets):
            if not getattr(dev.client, "is_connected", True):
                dev.status = "disconnected"; results.append(False); continue
            try:
                await dev.client.write_gatt_char(char_uuid or dev.char_uuid, data, response=response)
                dev.last_ok = True; results.append(True)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    await dev.client.write_gatt_char(char_uuid or dev.char_uuid, data, response=not response)
                    dev.last_ok = True; results.append(True)
                except Exception as e2:
                    dev.last_ok = False
                    if dev.status != "disconnected":
                        dev.status = "disconnected"
                        log_error(f"DROPPED: {dev.name} — {e2}")
                    results.append(False)
            if i < len(targets) - 1:
                await asyncio.sleep(self.send_delay_ms / 1000.0)
        self._last_command = (hex_s, self.label_input.text().strip() or "cmd") \
            if hasattr(self, "label_input") else (hex_s, "cmd")
        return any(results)

    # ---------------- REMOTE ----------------
    def _build_remote_tab(self):
        w = QWidget(); layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        top = QHBoxLayout()
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Profile:"))
        self.profile_combo = QComboBox()
        self.profile_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.refresh_remote)
        top.addWidget(self.profile_combo)
        for txt, slot in [("New", self.new_profile), ("Rename", self.rename_profile),
                          ("Delete", self.del_profile)]:
            b = QPushButton(txt); b.clicked.connect(slot); top.addWidget(b)
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Delay between devices (ms):"))
        self.delay_spin = QSpinBox(); self.delay_spin.setRange(40, 500)
        self.delay_spin.setValue(self.send_delay_ms)
        self.delay_spin.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: setattr(self, "send_delay_ms", v))
        top.addWidget(self.delay_spin)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        body = QHBoxLayout(); left = QVBoxLayout(); right = QVBoxLayout()

        scroll = QScrollArea(); scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.remote_container = QWidget()
        self.remote_grid = QVBoxLayout(self.remote_container)
        self.remote_grid.setAlignment(Qt.AlignTop)
        scroll.setWidget(self.remote_container); left.addWidget(scroll)

        wheel_box = QGroupBox("Color")
        wl = QVBoxLayout(wheel_box)
        self.color_wheel = ColorWheel()
        self.color_wheel.colorPicked.connect(self._on_color_picked)
        wl.addWidget(self.color_wheel)

        brow = QHBoxLayout()
        brow.addWidget(QLabel("Brightness:"))
        self.bright_slider = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal)
        self.bright_slider.setRange(100, 1200); self.bright_slider.setValue(1000)
        self.bright_slider.valueChanged.connect(self._on_brightness)
        brow.addWidget(self.bright_slider)
        wl.addLayout(brow)

        self.color_swatch = QLabel(); self.color_swatch.setFixedHeight(28)
        self.color_swatch.setStyleSheet("background:#ff0000; border-radius:4px;")
        wl.addWidget(self.color_swatch)

        self.strip_view = StripView(15, 10); self.strip_view.set_color(0, 0, 0)
        wl.addWidget(QLabel("Strip preview (15 × 10):"))
        wl.addWidget(self.strip_view)
        right.addWidget(wheel_box)

        body.addLayout(left, 3); body.addLayout(right, 2)
        layout.addLayout(body, 1)
        return w

    def _on_brightness(self):
        self.color_wheel.set_value(self.bright_slider.value() / 1000.0)

    def _on_color_picked(self, r, g, b):
        self.color_swatch.setStyleSheet(f"background:rgb({r},{g},{b}); border-radius:4px;")
        self.strip_view.set_color(r, g, b)
        bri = self.bright_slider.value()
        hex_s = mrstar_color(r, g, b, brightness=bri)
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(hex_s))

    def refresh_profile_combo(self):
        self.profile_combo.blockSignals(True)
        cur = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        self.profile_combo.clear()
        for p in self.db.get_profiles(): self.profile_combo.addItem(p["name"])
        idx = self.profile_combo.findText(cur)
        if idx >= 0: self.profile_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        self.profile_combo.blockSignals(False); self.refresh_remote()

    def new_profile(self):
        n, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "New profile", "Name:")
        if ok and n:
            try: self.db.create_profile(n); self.refresh_profile_combo()
            except sqlite3.IntegrityError: QMessageBox.warning(self, "Exists", "Exists.")

    def del_profile(self):
        n = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        if n and QMessageBox.question(self, "Delete", f"Delete '{n}'?") == QMessageBox.Yes:
            self.db.delete_profile(n); self.refresh_profile_combo(); self.refresh_remote()

    def rename_profile(self):
        old = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        new, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Rename", "New:", text=old)
        if ok and new and new != old:
            self.db.rename_profile(old, new); self.refresh_profile_combo()

    def refresh_remote(self):
        while self.remote_grid.count():
            it = self.remote_grid.takeAt(0); wdg = it.widget()
            if wdg: wdg.deleteLater()
        pid = self.db.get_profile_id(self.profile_combo.currentText())
        if not pid: return
        rows = self.db.get_remote_buttons(pid)
        groups = {}
        for r in rows: groups.setdefault(r["group_name"], []).append(r)
        for gname, items in groups.items():
            box = QGroupBox(gname); grid = QGridLayout(box)
            for i, r in enumerate(items):
                wrap = QWidget(); wl = QHBoxLayout(wrap); wl.setContentsMargins(0,0,0,0)
                btn = QPushButton(r["label"] + ("  ▸" if r["is_macro"] else ""))
                btn.setToolTip(f"Hex: {r['hex']}")
                if r["is_macro"]:
                    frames = json.loads(r["macro_frames"] or "[]")
                    d = r["macro_delay_ms"]
                    btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, f=frames, dd=d:
                                        asyncio.create_task(self._play_macro(f, dd)))
                else:
                    btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, h=r["hex"]:
                                        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(h)))
                btn.setContextMenuPolicy(Qt.CustomContextMenu)
                btn.customContextMenuRequested.connect(
                    lambda pos, rid=r["id"], b=btn: self._button_menu(pos, rid, b))
                wl.addWidget(btn); grid.addWidget(wrap, i//3, i%3)
            self.remote_grid.addWidget(box)

    def _button_menu(self, pos, bid, btn):
        m = QMenu(self)
        m.addAction("Edit", lambda: self._edit_button(bid))
        m.addAction("Clone", lambda: (self.db.clone_remote_button(bid), self.refresh_remote()))
        m.addAction("Delete", lambda: (self.db.delete_remote_button(bid), self.refresh_remote()))
        m.exec(btn.mapToGlobal(pos))

    def _edit_button(self, bid):
        row = self.db.get_remote_button(bid)
        if not row: return
        nl, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Edit", "Label:", text=row["label"])
        if not ok: return
        nh, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Edit", "Hex:", text=row["hex"])
        if not ok: return
        ng, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Edit", "Group:", text=row["group_name"])
        if not ok: return
        self.db.update_remote_button(bid, nl, nh, ng); self.refresh_remote()

    # ---------------- MUSIC ----------------
    def _build_music_tab(self):
        w = QWidget(); layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        src_box = QGroupBox("Source"); sl = QVBoxLayout(src_box)
        self.source_combo = QComboBox()
        self.source_combo.addItems(["Microphone", "System audio (loopback)", "Music file"])
        self.source_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._source_changed)
        sl.addWidget(self.source_combo)

        self.mic_row = QWidget(); ml = QHBoxLayout(self.mic_row); ml.setContentsMargins(0,0,0,0)
        ml.addWidget(QLabel("Input:"))
        self.mic_combo = QComboBox(); self.mic_combo.setMinimumWidth(400)
        ml.addWidget(self.mic_combo, 1)
        rmb = QPushButton("Refresh"); rmb.clicked.connect(self._populate_mics)
        ml.addWidget(rmb); sl.addWidget(self.mic_row)

        self.file_row = QWidget(); fl = QHBoxLayout(self.file_row); fl.setContentsMargins(0,0,0,0)
        self.file_label = QLabel("No files")
        load_btn = QPushButton("Load..."); load_btn.clicked.connect(self._load_files)
        self.shuffle_check = QCheckBox("Shuffle")
        next_btn = QPushButton("Next"); next_btn.clicked.connect(self._next_file)
        fl.addWidget(self.file_label, 1); fl.addWidget(load_btn)
        fl.addWidget(self.shuffle_check); fl.addWidget(next_btn)
        sl.addWidget(self.file_row); self.file_row.setVisible(False)
        self._playlist = []; self._playlist_idx = 0
        layout.addWidget(src_box)

        pat_box = QGroupBox("Pattern"); pl = QGridLayout(pat_box)
        pl.addWidget(QLabel("Pattern:"), 0, 0)
        self.pattern_combo = QComboBox()
        self.pattern_combo.addItems([
            "Rainbow wave (hue rotates)", "Pulse brightness", "Strobe on beat",
            "Beat flash colour", "Static pink/purple pulse"])
        pl.addWidget(self.pattern_combo, 0, 1)

        pl.addWidget(QLabel("Sensitivity:"), 1, 0)
        self.sens = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal); self.sens.setRange(1, 40); self.sens.setValue(12)
        pl.addWidget(self.sens, 1, 1)
        pl.addWidget(QLabel("Speed:"), 2, 0)
        self.speed = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal); self.speed.setRange(1, 40); self.speed.setValue(15)
        pl.addWidget(self.speed, 2, 1)
        pl.addWidget(QLabel("Brightness cap:"), 3, 0)
        self.bright = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal); self.bright.setRange(100, 1200); self.bright.setValue(1000)
        pl.addWidget(self.bright, 3, 1)
        layout.addWidget(pat_box)

        ctrl = QHBoxLayout()
        self.start_music_btn = QPushButton("▶ Start"); self.start_music_btn.clicked.connect(self._start_music)
        self.stop_music_btn = QPushButton("■ Stop"); self.stop_music_btn.clicked.connect(self._stop_music)
        self.stop_music_btn.setEnabled(False)
        ctrl.addWidget(self.start_music_btn); ctrl.addWidget(self.stop_music_btn)
        layout.addLayout(ctrl)
        self.music_status = QLabel("Idle"); layout.addWidget(self.music_status)
        self._populate_mics()
        return w

    def _source_changed(self, idx):
        self.mic_row.setVisible(idx in (0, 1)); self.file_row.setVisible(idx == 2)

    def _populate_mics(self):
        self.mic_combo.clear()
        if not load_audio(): return
        try:
            seen = set()
            for i, name, api in self.music.list_inputs():
                key = (name, api)
                if key in seen: continue
                seen.add(key)
                self.mic_combo.addItem(f"[{api}] {name}   (idx {i})", i)
        except Exception as e: log_error(f"populate mics: {e}")

    def _load_files(self):
        files, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileNames(self, "Audio", "", "Audio (*.wav *.flac *.ogg *.mp3)")
        if files:
            self._playlist = files; self._playlist_idx = 0
            self.file_label.setText(f"{len(files)} file(s): {os.path.basename(files[0])}")

    def _next_file(self):
        if not self._playlist: return
        import random
        if self.shuffle_check.isChecked(): self._playlist_idx = random.randrange(len(self._playlist))
        else: self._playlist_idx = (self._playlist_idx + 1) % len(self._playlist)
        self.file_label.setText(f"Now: {os.path.basename(self._playlist[self._playlist_idx])}")
        if self._music_active: self.music.stop(); self._start_file()

    def _start_file(self):
        if not self._playlist: return
        path = self._playlist[self._playlist_idx]
        try: self.music.start_file(path); self.statusBar().showMessage(f"Playing {os.path.basename(path)}")
        except Exception as e: log_error(f"start_file: {e}"); QMessageBox.warning(self, "File", str(e))

    def _start_music(self):
        if not load_audio(): QMessageBox.warning(self, "Audio", "sounddevice/numpy missing"); return
        idx = self.source_combo.currentIndex()
        try:
            if idx == 0:
                dev = self.mic_combo.currentData()
                if dev is None: QMessageBox.warning(self, "Mic", "No device."); return
                self.music.start_mic(dev)
            elif idx == 1: self.music.start_system_loopback()
            elif idx == 2: self._start_file()
            self._music_active = True
            self.start_music_btn.setEnabled(False); self.stop_music_btn.setEnabled(True)
            self.music_timer.start(); self.music_status.setText("Running")
        except Exception as e:
            log_error(f"start_music: {e}"); QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", str(e))

    def _stop_music(self):
        self._music_active = False
        try: self.music.stop()
        except Exception: pass
        self.music_timer.stop()
        self.start_music_btn.setEnabled(True); self.stop_music_btn.setEnabled(False)
        self.music_status.setText("Idle")

    def _music_tick(self):
        if not self._music_active: return
        if not any(d.status == "connected" for d in self.connected_devices.values()):
            self._stop_music(); self.music_status.setText("Stopped (no device)"); return
        try: self.music.analyse()
        except Exception as e: log_error(f"analyse: {e}"); return
        pat = self.pattern_combo.currentText()
        sens = self.sens.value() / 10.0
        speed = self.speed.value() / 10.0
        cap = self.bright.value()
        r = g = b = 0
        if pat == "Rainbow wave (hue rotates)":
            t = time.time() * speed * 0.3
            r, g, b = ColorWheel._hsv((t % 1.0), 1, 1)
        elif pat == "Pulse brightness":
            v = int(min(cap, self.music.rms * 2000 * sens))
            r = g = b = v
            # apply as brightness scale, keep hue neutral
            hex_s = mrstar_color(255, 255, 255, brightness=min(1200, v))
            asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(hex_s, response=False)); return
        elif pat == "Strobe on beat":
            v = cap if self.music.beat else 0
            hex_s = mrstar_color(255, 255, 255, brightness=max(1, v))
            asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(hex_s, response=False)); return
        elif pat == "Beat flash colour":
            if self.music.beat:
                hue = (time.time() * speed * 0.5) % 1.0
                r, g, b = ColorWheel._hsv(hue, 1, 1)
            else:
                r = g = b = 0
        elif pat == "Static pink/purple pulse":
            base = self.music.rms * sens
            r = int(min(255, 180 + base * 75))
            g = 0
            b = int(min(255, 100 + base * 100))
        # scale RGB by overall brightness cap
        scale = cap / 1000.0
        r = int(r * scale); g = int(g * scale); b = int(b * scale)
        r = max(0, min(255, r)); g = max(0, min(255, g)); b = max(0, min(255, b))
        hex_s = mrstar_color(r, g, b, brightness=1000)
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(hex_s, response=False))
        self.strip_view.set_color(r, g, b)
        self.music_status.setText(f"Running | RGB {r},{g},{b}")

    # ---------------- LAB ----------------
    def _build_lab_tab(self):
        w = QWidget(); layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        top = QHBoxLayout()
        self.hex_input = QLineEdit(); self.hex_input.setPlaceholderText("e.g. BC0406000003E8000055")
        self.label_input = QLineEdit(); self.label_input.setPlaceholderText("Label")
        self.response_check = QCheckBox("Write with response")
        send_btn = QPushButton("Send & Log"); send_btn.clicked.connect(self._send_and_log)
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Hex:")); top.addWidget(self.hex_input)
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Label:")); top.addWidget(self.label_input)
        top.addWidget(self.response_check); top.addWidget(send_btn)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        self.log_table = QTableWidget(0, 6)
        self.log_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["ID","Time","Device","Char","Hex","Label"])
        self.log_table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectRows)
        layout.addWidget(self.log_table)

        btns = QHBoxLayout()
        for txt, slot in [("Refresh", self.refresh_log), ("Clear Log", self.clear_log),
                          ("Diff", self.diff_selected), ("Add to Remote", self.add_selected_to_remote)]:
            b = QPushButton(txt); b.clicked.connect(slot); btns.addWidget(b)
        layout.addLayout(btns)

        # Quick color builder
        test_box = QGroupBox("Quick color (RGB → BC0406, MR Star)")
        tl = QGridLayout(test_box)
        tl.addWidget(QLabel("R:"), 0, 0); self.qr = QSpinBox(); self.qr.setRange(0,255); self.qr.setValue(255)
        tl.addWidget(self.qr, 0, 1)
        tl.addWidget(QLabel("G:"), 0, 2); self.qg = QSpinBox(); self.qg.setRange(0,255); self.qg.setValue(0)
        tl.addWidget(self.qg, 0, 3)
        tl.addWidget(QLabel("B:"), 0, 4); self.qb = QSpinBox(); self.qb.setRange(0,255); self.qb.setValue(0)
        tl.addWidget(self.qb, 0, 5)
        tl.addWidget(QLabel("Brightness:"), 1, 0)
        self.qbri = QSpinBox(); self.qbri.setRange(1, 1200); self.qbri.setValue(1000)
        tl.addWidget(self.qbri, 1, 1)
        send_rgb = QPushButton("Send")
        send_rgb.clicked.connect(self._send_rgb_test)
        tl.addWidget(send_rgb, 1, 2)
        layout.addWidget(test_box)

        macro_box = QGroupBox("Macro / Scroll")
        ml = QVBoxLayout(macro_box)
        self.macro_text = QTextEdit(); ml.addWidget(self.macro_text)
        mrow = QHBoxLayout()
        self.macro_delay = QSpinBox(); self.macro_delay.setRange(10, 2000); self.macro_delay.setValue(55)
        self.macro_delay.setSuffix(" ms")
        for txt, slot in [("Load Scroll", self._load_scroll),
                          ("Save as Button", self._save_macro),
                          ("Play", lambda: asyncio.create_task(self._play_macro_from_text()))]:
            b = QPushButton(txt); b.clicked.connect(slot); mrow.addWidget(b)
        mrow.insertWidget(0, self.macro_delay)
        ml.addLayout(mrow); layout.addWidget(macro_box)
        return w

    def _send_rgb_test(self):
        r, g, b = self.qr.value(), self.qg.value(), self.qb.value()
        bri = self.qbri.value()
        hex_s = mrstar_color(r, g, b, brightness=bri)
        self.hex_input.setText(hex_s)
        self.label_input.setText(f"RGB {r},{g},{b} @ {bri}")
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_and_log())

    def _send_and_log(self): asyncio.create_task(self.send_and_log())

    async def send_and_log(self):
        h = self.hex_input.text().strip(); l = self.label_input.text().strip()
        if not h: return
        ok = await self.send_hex(h, response=self.response_check.isChecked())
        if ok:
            for dev in self.connected_devices.values():
                if dev.status == "connected":
                    self.db.add_command(dev.address, dev.char_uuid, h, l)
            self.refresh_log()

    def refresh_log(self):
        rows = self.db.get_commands()
        self.log_table.setRowCount(len(rows))
        for i, r in enumerate(rows):
            for j, k in enumerate(["id","ts","device_address","char_uuid","hex","label"]):
                self.log_table.setItem(i, j, QTableWidgetItem(str(r[k] or "")))
        self.log_table.resizeColumnsToContents()

    def clear_log(self):
        if QMessageBox.question(self, "Clear", "Delete all?") == QMessageBox.Yes:
            self.db.clear_commands(); self.refresh_log()

    def diff_selected(self):
        sel = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if len(sel) != 2: return
        rows = [(self.log_table.item(i.row(), 5).text(), self.log_table.item(i.row(), 4).text()) for i in sel]
        a = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", rows[0][1]))
        b = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", rows[1][1]))
        lines = [f"{rows[0][0]} vs {rows[1][0]}", ""]
        for i in range(max(len(a), len(b))):
            av = f"{a[i]:02X}" if i < len(a) else "--"
            bv = f"{b[i]:02X}" if i < len(b) else "--"
            lines.append(f"{i:03d}: {av}  {bv}{'  ' if av==bv else ' *'}")
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Diff", "\n".join(lines))

    def add_selected_to_remote(self):
        sel = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if not sel: return
        pid = self.db.get_profile_id(self.profile_combo.currentText())
        group, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Group", "Group:", text="Colors")
        if not ok: group = "Ungrouped"
        for i in sel:
            r = i.row()
            self.db.add_remote_button(pid, self.log_table.item(r,5).text() or "Btn",
                                      self.log_table.item(r,4).text(),
                                      self.log_table.item(r,3).text(), group)
        self.refresh_remote()

    def _load_scroll(self): self.macro_text.setPlainText("\n".join(SCROLL_MACRO))

    def _save_macro(self):
        frames = [x.strip() for x in self.macro_text.toPlainText().splitlines() if x.strip()]
        if not frames: return
        label, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Name", "Name:", text="Macro")
        if not ok or not label: return
        pid = self.db.get_profile_id(self.profile_combo.currentText())
        self.db.add_remote_button(pid, label, frames[0], None, "Macros",
                                  is_macro=1, macro_frames=json.dumps(frames),
                                  macro_delay_ms=self.macro_delay.value())
        self.refresh_remote()

    async def _play_macro_from_text(self):
        frames = [x.strip() for x in self.macro_text.toPlainText().splitlines() if x.strip()]
        await self._play_macro(frames, self.macro_delay.value())

    async def _play_macro(self, frames, delay_ms):
        for f in frames:
            if not self.connected_devices or self._closing: break
            if not any(d.status == "connected" for d in self.connected_devices.values()): break
            await self.send_hex(f, response=False)
            await asyncio.sleep(delay_ms / 1000.0)

    # ---------------- LAYOUT ----------------
    def _build_layout_tab(self):
        w = QWidget(); layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Live preview of every connected strip."))
        self.layout_area = QVBoxLayout()
        holder = QWidget(); holder.setLayout(self.layout_area)
        scroll = QScrollArea(); scroll.setWidgetResizable(True); scroll.setWidget(holder)
        layout.addWidget(scroll)
        self.layout_views = {}
        self.layout_timer = QTimer(); self.layout_timer.setInterval(800)
        self.layout_timer.timeout.connect(self._refresh_layout); self.layout_timer.start()
        return w

    def _refresh_layout(self):
        current = set(self.connected_devices.keys())
        shown = set(self.layout_views.keys())
        if current != shown:
            while self.layout_area.count():
                it = self.layout_area.takeAt(0); wdg = it.widget()
                if wdg: wdg.deleteLater()
            self.layout_views.clear()
            for addr, dev in self.connected_devices.items():
                box = QGroupBox(f"{dev.name}  —  {addr}  [{dev.status}]")
                bl = QVBoxLayout(box)
                sv = StripView(15, 10); bl.addWidget(sv)
                bl.addWidget(QLabel(f"Char: {dev.char_uuid}"))
                self.layout_area.addWidget(box)
                self.layout_views[addr] = sv
        base = self.strip_view.base
        for sv in self.layout_views.values():
            sv.set_color(base.red(), base.green(), base.blue())

    # ---------------- SHUTDOWN ----------------
    def closeEvent(self, event):
        self._closing = True
        for t in (self.music_timer, self.layout_timer, self.heartbeat_timer):
            try: t.stop()
            except Exception: pass
        try: self.music.stop()
        except Exception: pass
        if self.connected_devices:
            try:
                loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
                async def dc():
                    for dev in list(self.connected_devices.values()):
                        try: await dev.client.disconnect()
                        except Exception: pass
                fut = asyncio.ensure_future(dc())
                deadline = time.time() + 3.0
                while not fut.done() and time.time() < deadline:
                    try: loop.run_until_complete(asyncio.sleep(0.05))
                    except Exception: break
            except Exception: pass
            self.connected_devices.clear()
        try: self.db.close()
        except Exception: pass
        event.accept()


def main():
    sys.excepthook = excepthook
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    loop = qasync.QEventLoop(app); asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)
    win = MainWindow()
    icon_path = find_icon()
    if icon_path:
        icon = QIcon(icon_path)
        if not icon.isNull():
            app.setWindowIcon(icon); win.setWindowIcon(icon)
            log_error(f"Icon: loaded {icon_path}")
    win.show()
    try:
        with loop: loop.run_forever()
    except Exception as e:
        log_error(f"crash: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()}"); raise

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Good, this is the right call. Let's nail the format before touching code.

What we know for certain
Format: BC 04 06 A A B B C C 55 (10 bytes)

A A = hue, 0–360 big-endian. Confirmed: 0000=red, 003C=yellow, 0078=green, 00F0=blue.

B B = "value" — but here's the problem you spotted

C C = always 00 00 in your verified scroll frames, meaning unknown

What "pale green" tells us
When you lower B B you get green mixed with white. That is exactly what happens when a color system's saturation drops — the color loses purity and mixes with the white LEDs.

Two theories:

Theory 1 — B B controls saturation, not brightness.
There's a hidden separate brightness channel we haven't found. This would explain why dimming always looks washed out.

Theory 2 — B B controls both saturation AND brightness at once.
The strip fuses them. Then to get real dim colors you need a different command for brightness — maybe BC 06 02 (which we saw in the capture, and never tested properly).

Test these 6 commands, one at a time
Same strip, 2 seconds apart. Report the exact visual result of each.

text
1. BC0406007803E8000055    (known green — baseline)
2. BC0406007801F4000055    (green at half value — pale? dark?)
3. BC0406007800C8000055    (green at quarter value)
4. BC0406007803E801F455    (green, value maxed, C C = 01F4)
5. BC0406007801F403E855    (green, value half, C C = 03E8)
6. BC0406007803E8FFFF55    (green, value maxed, C C = FFFF)
What each answer means:

If #4 and #6 change brightness → C C is the real brightness channel, and we've been treating it as padding.

If #2 and #3 are pale but stay same brightness → B B is saturation, brightness is elsewhere.

If #2 and #3 get darker (not paler) → B B is brightness, and "pale" was just a perception thing at very low values.

If #5 is paler than #4 → C C is not brightness.

If nothing changes vs #1 → C C is truly unused and there's a hidden brightness command we haven't found.

v1.1 — MR Star Final
Two findings locked in:

Pure color = last two bytes FF FF (or 01 F4). Anything else mixes in white. Full format: BC 04 06 [HH HH] [BB BB] [WW WW] 55 where WW = FFFF for pure, 0000 for soft/whitened.

Effects row: BC 06 02 00 XX 55 are M+ patterns (blank-space, white-space, crawl, etc.) — I'm exposing them as clickable buttons so you can discover them all.

Plus: Console (multi-line batch with per-line delay) and Event Log tab (live, filterable, saveable).
